FORD, J.—b. 1910; ed. Latymer Upper Sch. and Oxford Univ.; mil. serv., 1940-43, capt.; entom., tsetse res. dept., 1938; tsetse res. offr., E.A.H.C., 1948; dep. dir., 1952.

FORREST, J. W.—b. 1908; ed. Biggar High Sch. and Univ. of Glasgow; educ. offr., St. V., 1943; dir., educ., Br. Hond., 1947; senr. educ. offr., Gam., 1950; dir., educ., 1952.

FORRESTER, H.—b. 1903; ed. Beath Acad.; inspr. wts. and measures, 1930; collr., customs, Tang., 1943; tyre and petrol contrlr., 1942-44; senr. collr., customs, E.A.H.C., 1949; compt., customs and excise, Som., 1950.

FORSYTH, C. R.—b. 1910; ed. Ballarat High Sch., Melbourne, and Oxford Univ.; mil. serv., 1942-45, lieut. (p.o.w.); cadet, M.C.S., 1936; asst. imm. comsrr. for Mal., Negapatam, S. India, 1937; cl. V., 1939; cl. IV., 1943; cl. III., 1947; p.a.s., S'pore., 1950; Imp. Def. Coll., 1951; cl. IC., 1951; cl. IB., 1952; ag. sec. treasy., F. of Mal., 1954.

FORTE, P. A.—b. 1914; ed. Queen's Coll.; appren., lands and mines dept., B. Guiana, 1932; govt. survr., 1942; asst. dist. comsrr., 1945; dist. comsrr., 1947; senr., 1953; topographer on geol. surv., 1934-40.

FORWARD, A. A., O.B.E. (Mil.)—b. 1906; ed. Purcell House, Scarborough; mil. serv., 1940-46; comsrr., road transport, Mal., 1946.

FOSTER, W. N.—b. 1900; ed. Dulwich Coll. and Pembroke Coll., Camb.; asst. petroleum tech., Trin., 1936; petroleum tech., 1946.

FOSTER-SUTTON, Sir Stafford, K.C.M.G. (1951), C.M.G. (1948), O.B.E. (mil.) FOX-STRANGWAYS, V., C.B.E. (1953).—b. 1898; ed. Winchester; mil. serv., 1916-22, capt., and 1940-45, col.; cadet, Nig., 1923; attd. FRANCIS, K. M.—b. 1913; ed. Univs. of Wales and Lond.; meteorologist, E.A., 1938; statistician, 1943; C.O., 1947; govt. statistician, S.L., 1951; G.C., 1952.

FRANKSON, A. S.—b. 1914; ed. Mico Training Coll., J'ca, Inst. of Educ., Univ. of Lond.; supvr., schs., B. Hond., 1937; dist. comsrr., 1953; mem., comtee. to inquire into juvenile delinquency in B. Hond., 1952.

FRENCH, M. H., O.B.E. (1951).—b. 1909; ed. Cheltenham Gram. FRISBY, A. W., C.B.E. (1952), E.D. (1946).—b. 1897; ed. Northampton Gram. Sch., Oxford and Lond. (Chinese), 1946; dir., S’pore, 1948; ret., re-apptd. chmn., publ. servs. comsn., 1952; M.L.C., 1949-52; mem. of council, Univ. of Mal.

FRYE, D. A.—b. 1915; ed. Cheltenham Gram. Sch., Imp. Coll. of Science, Lond.; mil. serv. and p.o.w., 1941-45; asst. gov’t. analyst, S.S., 1938; title changed to chief chmst., 1939; chmst., customs and excise, Mal., 1946; chmst., 1947; senr. chmst., Penang, 1949; ch. chmst., S’pore, 1953.

FUCHS, V. E.—b. 1908; ed. Brighton Coll. and St. John’s Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1939-47, maj. Dependencies sci. bureau, 1950; numerous sci. pp. on geog. and geol. of E.A. and the Antarctic.

FUDGE, B. T.—b. 1913; ed. St. Bart’s C. of E. Sch., Sydenham, and Camberwell Sch. of Arts and Crafts; A.F.S./nat. fire serv., 1939-44; asst. supt., printing dept., Mal., 1947; supt., 1949; secon., Sarawak, 1952; dep. gov’t. printer, Fed. of Mal., 1953.

FUNG KEE FUNG, C. O.—b. 1920; ed. Queen’s Coll., B. Guiana, and Lond. Univ.; asst. surg. spec., publ. hosp., Georgetown, 1948; surg. spec., 1949.

GABAY, A. L.—b. 1904; ed. Cornwall Coll.; first appt., J’ca, 1925; examiner of accts., audit dept., 1940; dep. audr. gen., 1951.

GABOUREL, R. E., M.B.E.—b. 1896; ed. St. Mary’s Sch. and Diocesan High Sch., Belize; apptd. coll. serv., B. Hond., 1913; asst. col. sec., 1942.

GADISHAW, E. A.—b. 1897; ed. Wolmers Sch., J’ca; mil. serv., 1916-19; asst., treasy., J’ca, 1919; 2nd cl. clk., 1921; 1st cl., 1933; examr. of accts., 1939; dep. audr. gen., 1941; audr. gen., 1950; served on number of public comtees. of enquiry.

GAILER, J. W.—b. 1914; ed. Wigan and Dist. Tech. Coll.; dep. dir., educ. (tech.), Uga., 1953.

GALEA, L., O.B.E. (1950).—b. 1895; ed. St. Ignatius and St. Aloysius Coll. and Royal Univ. of Malta; advoc. for the poor and curator ex-officio, Malta, 1930-36; nom. mem. coun. of govt., 1939-40; secon. reg. protection offr., 1940-41; atty-gen., 1941; M.E.C., 1941-47; supplementary judge, 1929-32; chmn. public serv. comsn., 1947.

GALLEY, R. A. E.—b. 1909; ed. Colfe’s Gram. Sch. and Imp. Coll. of Sci. and Tech. GALSWORTHY, A. N., C.M.G. (1953).—b. 1916; ed. Emanuel Sch. and Corpus Christi Coll., Camb.; apptd. after compet. exam., asst. prin., C.O., 1938; prin., 1946; asst. sec., 1947; ch. sec., W.A.I.T.C., 1951; C.O., 1954.

GASKELL, W. P., M.B.E. (1953).—b. 1914; ed. Dragon Sch., Oxford, Blundell's Sch. and Camborne Sch. of Mines; mil. serv., 1940-43, capt.; inspr. of mines, Nig., 1937; senr. inspr. 1947; ast. ch. inspr., 1947; dep. ch. 1949; ch. inspr., 1952.

GIBBONS, E. J., C.B.E. (mil.) (1947), M.B.E. (civ.) (1939).—b. 1906; ed. King Henry VIII Sch., Coventry, and Gonville and Caius Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1941-46, brig. GIBBS, C. B.—b. 1910; ed. Grenada Boys' Sec. Is., 1944; govt. sec., St. V., 1944; ast. adminr., St. V., 1951; Grenada, 1953; mem. bd. of inc. tax comsrs., 1947.

GIBSON, G.—b. 1902; ast. loco. supt., Nig., 1925; wks. man., T.R. & P.S., 1937; ch. mech. engr., 1939; ast. ch., K.U.R. & H., 1946; ch. mech. engr., E.A.R. & H., 1948.

GIBSON, W.—b. 1906; ed. McKenna Memorial and Gram. Sch., Larne; Merchant Navy, 1922-37; R.A.F., 1937-41; nav. serv., 1941-46, lt.-comdr.; marine offr., S'pore, 1946; marine supt., N. Borneo, 1946.

GIDDEN, B. O. B.—b. 1915; ed. King Edward VI Sch., Southampton, and Jesus Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1939-45, maj.; appt. after compet. exam., ast. prin., H.M.O. of wks., 1939; p.s. to perm. sec., 1945; prin., 1946; p.s. to min. of wks., 1946-48; prin., C.O., 1949; ast. sec., 1951; counsellor (col. affrs.) to U.K. del. to U.N., N. York, 1954.
GILBERT, C. G. G., O.B.E., M.C.—b. 1893; ed. Saltus Gram. Sch., Berm., Dulwich Coll. and Oxford Univ.; mil. serv., 1915-19; maj.; dir. educ., Berm., 1924; mem. educ. comsnr., 1929.

GILBERT, J. O.—b. 1907; ed. Felsted and Pembroke Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1942-46; maj.; cadet, Sarawak C.S., 1928; dist. offr., 1938; res., 1946; senr. res., secon. Br. res., Brunei, 1954.

GILBERT, Sir Joseph, Kt. Bach. (1955), C.B.E. (1949), O.B.E., Q.C.—b. 1888; ed. Bedford and Brasenose Coll., Oxford; asst. coll., Zanz., 1912; asst. sec. and p.s. to res., 1915; senr. advsry. sec., 1924; asst. ch. sec., 1928; del., C.O. conf., 1930; ret., 1934; atty.-gen., Berm., 1938; ch. just., 1952.

GILBERT, W. T. M.—b. 1900; ed. Rathmines Sch., Dublin; med. offr., Nig., 1930; S.M.O., 1949; asst. D.M.S., G.C., 1951; prin. M.O., 1953.

GILL, D. R. B.—b. 1914; ed. St. Michael's Coll. and Wesley Coll., Belize; junr. clk., B. Hond., 1933; asst. acctnt., 1950; senr. acctnt. (later acctnt.-gen.), 1952.

GILL, H. C.—b. 1903; ed. Charterhouse and Christ Church, Oxford; barrister-at-law; cadet, Nig., 1925; senr. dist. offr., 1945; admin. offr., cl. I, 1948.

GILL, H. S. C.—b. 1903; ed. Royal High Sch., Edin. Univ. and Christ Church, Oxford; cadet, Tang., 1927; dist. offr., 1929; dep. prov. comsnr., 1946; prov. comsnr., 1950; senr., 1954.

GILLES, E. C.—b. 1901; ed. St. Thomas' Coll., Colombo, Ceylon Medical Coll., Royal Infirmary, Edin. and Johns Hopkins Univ., U.S.A.; M.O.H., Ceylon, 1926; Nig., 1937; senr. health offr., 1946; asst. D.M.S., 1947; D.D.M.S., 1949; D.M.S., N. regn., 1951; author of numerous articles in technical journals, etc., on health matters.

GILLETTE, H. P. S.—b. 1904; ed. St. Mary's Coll., Trin., and Edin. Univ.; med. serv., Wind. Is., 1928; port health offr., St. L., 1934; M.O.H., Trin., 1939; malariologist, 1944; later suptg. M.O., specialist, Malaria div., pres. of med. bd., 1947; author and co-author of various publns. on malaria.

GILLMAN, H.—b. 1908; ed. Dale Coll., S.A., Univs. of S.A., Camb. and Witwatersrand., and I.C.T.A.; agric. asst., Tang., 1933; agric. offr., 1934; senr., 1948; dep. dir., agric., Nyasa, 1951.

GITTENS, B. B.—b. 1916; ed. Grenada Boys' Sec. Sch.; apptd. to civil serv., treasy., 1938; various depts., 1941-53; examr. of accts., 1954.

GITTENS, C. D.—b. 1906; ed. Holy Trinity and Lynch's Secondary Schs., Barb.; cadet, col. sec's off., Barb., 1924; asst. audr. gen., 1950; sec., nutrn. comtee., 1936-37.

GITTENS, E. S.—b. 1911; ed. Grenada Boys' Sec. Sch and Oxford Univ.; clk. treasy., Grenada, 1931; agric. dept., 1932; col. sec's. off., 1937; sec., agric. bd., land sett. comtee., econ. advsry. comtee., clk. exec. and leg. couns., 1942; clk. leg. coun., 1944-46; sec. for dev., 1947; clk., exec. coun., govt. sec., med. registr. and man., govt. printery, 1949.

GLASGOW, E. F.—b. 1920; ed. St. Mary's Coll., St. L.; barrister-at-law, Gray's Inn; legal asst., Grenada, 1949; mag., St. V., 1952.

GLENWAY, Sir Vincent, K.C.M.G. (1942), C.M.G. (1937), O.B.E. (1929)—b. 1891; ed. St. Bee's Sch., Cumberland and Wadham Coll., Oxford; asst. dist. comsnr., E.A.P., 1913; offr. in ch., Somali disarmament, N. Frontier dist., 1919 (A.G.S. med.); secon. as dist. comsnr., Br. Som., 1920; asst. dist. comsnr., 1922; dist. comsnr., 1926; Abyssinian miss., 1927-28; senr. dist. comsnr., 1934; prov. comsnr., 1935; gov. and c-in-c., Som., 1939; C.O., 1942-43; Br. agt., E. Aden Prot., and res. advr., Hadramaut States, 1944-45; Br. res., Zanz., 1946-52; speaker, E.A. cent. leg. assembly., 1953.

GLENNIE, A. F. B.—b. 1903; ed. Royal Naval Colls., Osborne, and Dartmouth and Trinity Coll., Camb.; cadet, N. Rhod., 1924; asst. native comsnr., 1927; dist. offr., gr. III, 1929; gr. II, 1934; prov. comsnr., 1945.

GOBLE, L. H., C.M.G. (1953).—b. 1901; ed. King's Sch., Canterbury, and R.M.C., Sandhurst; mil. serv., 1920-23, lieut.; cadet, Nig., 1924; cl. II, admin. offr., 1943; cl. I, 1945; staff gr., 1947; sec., N. provs., 1947; admin. sec., 1952; mem., bd. of control, Gaskiya corp., 1945-51.

GOCKING, W. E.—b. 1910; ed. Queen's Royal Coll., Trin., Univ. Coll., Lond.; 4th cl. clk., Trin., 1931; 3rd cl., 1935; senr., 1937; prin. offr., sec't, 1940; secon. to central library servs., 1945; librarian, 1949; sec., various comtees.; clk., leg. co., 1937-38.

GOMES, S. E., Q.C.—b. 1901; ed. St. Joseph's Coll., Dumfries, and Jesus Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law; mag., B. Guiana, 1929; asst. atty.-gen., 1933; atty.-gen., Leeward Is., 1944; puisne judge, Trin., 1948; senr., 1954; comsnr. to enq. into the affairs of Helena country dist., 1928, Skelton explosion, 1940; chmn., comsnr. to enq. into affrs. of P. of Spain Corp., 1951.

GONSALVES, N. H.—b. 1902; mil. serv., 1944-47; asst. supt., central lunatic asylum, Antigua, 1931; ch. cotton inspr. and forestry offr., 1939; supt., prisons, St. L., 1948; St. V., 1954.

GOOCH, S. J. W., C.B.E. (1954).—b. 1889; asst. engrnr., P.W.D., F.M.S., 1912; exec. engrnr., Johore, 1922; senr. exec. engrnr., P.W.D., 1927; senr. exec. engrnr., hydraulics, Selangor, 1929; res. engrnr., civ. aerodrome, S'pore, 1933; senr. exec. engrnr., Kinta, 1933; state engrnr., gr. I, 1935; Perak, 1936; D.D.P.W., S.S., 1938; D.P.W., Nig., 1939; senr. temp. tech. offr., C.A., 1945; dep. ch. civ. engrnr., 1948; ch. civ. engrnr., 1949.
GOODBAN, J. W. D.—b. 1915; ed. Bradfield Coll., Christ's Coll., Camb., and I.C.T.A.; agric. offr., Nig., 1940; senr., 1948; dir. of agric., B. Hond., 1951.

GOODE, A. N.—b. 1913; ed. Charterhouse and Camb. Univ.; mil. serv., 1941; cadet, F.M.S., 1936; cl. V, 1939; cl. IV, 1943; p.s. to high commnr., 1946; cl. III, 1947; asst. fin. sec., 1948; cl. IC, 1951; cl. IB, 1952; fin. sec., N. Borneo, 1953.

GOODE, W. A. C., C.M.G. (1952).—b. 1907; ed. Oakham Sch. and Worcester Coll., Oxford; barrister-at-law, Gray's Inn; mobilized 1st Batt., S'pore Vol. Corps, 1941; p.o.w., 1942-45; apptd. M.C.S., 1931; asst. sec. to Br. res., Selangor, 1932; asst. advr., Besut, 1933; dist. offr., Raub, 1936; asst. fin. sec., S'pore, 1939; asst. commnr. for civ. def., Mal., 1940; prin. asst. sec., 1946; dep. econ. sec., 1948; ch. sec., Aden, 1949; col. sec., S'pore, 1953.

GOODWIN, D. I.—b. 1914; ed. Brighton Gram. Sch., Lond. IC, state dev. offr., Pahang, 1952; dep. commnr., labr., S'pore, 1953.

GOODWIN, F. W.—b. 1912; ed. Ken.; apptd. Tang., 1931; proof reader, 1936; junr. asst. press supt., 1940; asst. supt., 1945; Zanz., 1949; govt. printer, 1951; Aden, 1955.

GORDON, G. C. L.—b. 1915; co-op. organizer, B. Guiana, 1945; asst. regis., co-op. soc., 1949; commnr. for co-op. dev., 1950.

GORDON, G. M., O.B.E. (1945).—b. 1885; ed. Trinity Coll., Dublin; mil. serv., 1915-20 (desps.); mil. and civ. wks., Mesopotamia, also Samawa Relief Force, 1920-22; D.P.W., Bahamas, 1923; D.D.P.W., Trin., 1928; construction engrn., D.W.W.I., 1948; engngrg. advr., 1951.

GORDON, G. W.—b. 1902; ed. Queen's Royal Coll., Trin.; survr., P.W.D., Trin., 1930; senr. survr., 1943; dep. dir., survs. and dep. sub-intendant of crown lands, 1948; dir., 1952.

GORDON, K. L.—b. 1906; ed. St. Mary's Coll., St. L.; barrister-at-law; police mag., Grenada, 1938; crown atty., Dominica, 1942; mag., Trin., 1943; puisne judge, Wind. and Leew'd Is., 1954.

GORDON, N. L.—b. 1903; ed. St. George's Coll., Oak Grove Sch., Mussoorie, India, Univs. of the Punjab and Lond.; mil. serv., 1940-47, lt.-col.; Indian serv., inc. tax offr., 1927; col. serv., assessment offr., Nig., 1948; dep. commnr. of inc. tax, S.L., 1949; commnr., 1950.

GORDON, W. A.—b. 1910; ed. Sedbergh, Trinity Coll., Oxford; barrister-at-law, Inner Temple; mil. serv., 1939-44, capt.; asst. consvtr., forests, G.C., 1935; Cyp., 1939; consvtr., B. Guiana, 1952.

GORMAN, W. J., B.E.M., Q.F.S.M.—b. 1896; ed. High Sch.; war serv., 1942, flt. lieut.; dir., fire servs., Mal., 1946; ch. offr., fire bde., H.K., 1949; numerous published papers relating to fire, 1940-42.

GOSLING, Alice E., M.B.E. (1952).—b. 1897; ed. Bermuda High Sch. for Girls and privately; Berm. library, 1928; librarian, 1940.

GOULD, T. J.—b. 1906; ed. Auckland Gram. Sch. and Univ. of N.Z.; barrister and solr., N.Z. and Fiji; mil. serv., 1941-45; p.o.w., 1942-45; cr. coun., H.K., 1938; marshal in prize, 1939; prop. offr. of crown, 1940-41; temp. addl. judge, 1946-47; puisne judge, 1948.

GRACIE, A. J.—b. 1904; ed. Royal High Sch. and Univ., Edin.; cadet, F.M.S., 1928; cl. V, 1931; D.O., 1934; cl. IV, 1935; cl. III, 1940; cl. II, 1945; cl. IB, 1948; estab. offr., staff gr. A, Mal., 1949; supernum. offr., serv. branch, 1953; fedl. estab. offr., 1954.

GRAHAM, D. D.—b. 1911; ed. Govan High Sch. and Paisley Tech. Coll.; interned, 1942-45; survr. of ships, S.S., 1938; M. of Trans. course, 1938-39; senr. ship survr., S'pore, 1946; survr-gen. of ships, 1952; advr. to govt. of Mal. on marine survs., 1952; survr-gen., ships, Mal., in addn., 1953.

GRAHAM, R. M.—b. 1898; ed. St. Andrews Coll., R.M.C., Sandhurst, and Keble Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1919-22, capt.; forest serv., Ken., 1925; senr. asst. consvtr., forests (later consvtr.), 1951.

GRANGE, E.—b. 1908; H.M. prison service, 1935-39 and 1942-43; asst. supt., prisons, S.L., 1939; police, 1943; supt., prisons, Zanz., 1949.

GRANNUM, F. N.—b. 1896; ed. Harrison Coll., Barb., Edin., Univ., L.S.T.M. and H. and Harvard Univ.; Barb., V. F. (surg. capt.); police med. offr., dist. F., Barb., 1931; sanitation offr., 1936; S.M.O. of H., 1950.

GRANT, G. A.—b. 1909; ed. St. V. Gram. Sch.; clk., elec. dept., St. V., 1931; clk., P.W.D., 1934; man., govt. cotton ginney, 1936; attd. Trin. govt. surveys dept. (field train.), 1941; asst. supt., wks., 1942; supt., wks. and crown land, St. V., 1944; dir., wks., Dominica, 1952.

GRANTHAM, Sir Alexander, G.C.M.G. (1951), K.C.M.G. (1945), C.M.G. (1941).—b. 1899; ed. Wellington, R.M.C. and Pembroke Coll., Camb.; admin. offr., H.K., 1922; col. sec., Berm., 1935; Jca., 1938; ch. sec., Nig., 1941; gov., Fiji, and high commnr., W. Pac., 1945; gov. and c.in-c., H.K., 1947.

GRANTHAM, G. J.—b. 1915; ed. Stonyhurst Coll. and Pembroke Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1939-46, maj.; asst. audr., Ken., 1946; prin. audr., B. Hond., 1950; senr. audr., Nig., 1953.
GRATTAN-BELLEW, A. J., Q.C. (1952).—b. 1903; ed. Downside and Christ's Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law, Lincoln's Inn; mil. serv., 1941-42; p.o.w., 1942-45; mem. leg. dept., Egyptian govt., 1936-38; col. leg. serv., 1938; mag., F.M.S., 1938; asst. leg. advr. and dep. public prosecutor, 1939; cr. (later fedl.) coun., Mal., 1946; atty.-gen., Sarawak, 1948; atty.-gen., and mem. for leg. affrs., Tang., 1952.

GRAY, D.—b. 1906; ed. Carlisle Gram. Sch., Merton Coll., Oxford, and Lond. Sch. of Econ. and Political Science; interned, 1942-45; cadet, S.S., 1930; att. immigr. dept., S'pore, 1933; cl. V, 1933; cl. IV, 1937; cl. III, 1942; dep. comsnr., labr., (H.Q.), cl. II, 1947; cl. IB, 1949; staff offr., gr. A, sec., Chinese affrs., Mal., 1951.

GRAY, G. L., O.B.E. (1953).—b. 1905; ed. Latymer (Upper) Sch.; asst. collr., land rev., and asst. protector (Chartered Co.'s serv.), N. GREAVES, E. G. N.—b. 1913; ed. in W. Indies and Lond. Univ.; temp. chem., dept. of govt. chemist, London, 1940; dep. govt. chem., Trin., 1945; govt. chem., 1950; served on copra prods. and explosives comtees., Trin.

GREAVES-HILL, C.—b. 1905; ed. J'ca Coll.; entered J'ca govt. serv., 1923; asst. labr. offr., 1941; gr. I, 1943; prin. clk., sec't., 1946; asst. fedl. labr. offr., Leeward Is., 1947; asst. labr. advr., J'ca, 1949; dep., 1950; secon. Br. consulate, Panama, as labr. repve. of govt. of J'ca, 1943-44.

GREEN, F. R. H.—b. 1901; ed. Highgate Sch.; Royal Sch. of Mines; inspr. of mines, Ken., 1933; supt., survs., B. Guiana, 1936; comsnr., lands and mines, 1939; ch. inspr., mines, S.L., 1947.

GREEN, R. P.—b. 1907; ed. Oundle Sch.; and Magdalene Coll., Cambridge; asst. audr., Nig., 1930; Mal., 1935; audr., Leeward Is., 1938; senr. asst. audr., Nig., 1944; Nyasa., 1946; dep. dir., aud., Tang., 1948; dir. N. Rhod., 1951; secon. to fedl. govt., 1954.

GREENE, L. R.—b. 1909; ed. Dublin Univ.; barrister-at-law, King's Inn, Dublin; mil. serv., 1940-41; cr. coun., Tang., 1935; res. mag., Zanz., 1937; jt. ed. Zanz. law reports, Vols. VI and VII, Brill. Star (4th cl.), Zanz.

GREENING, L. S., O.B.E., M.C. (1917), bar (1918).—b. 1895; mil. serv., 1914-18; capt., and 1940-45; lt.-col.; asst. inspr., police, Tang., 1920; cadet, 1925; asst. dist. offr., 1927; admin. offr., Cyp., 1935, sent., 1939; comsnr., Cyp. GREENWOOD, A. F., O.B.E.—b. 1905; ed. Woodhouse Grove Sch. and St. John's Coll., Camb.; asst. solir., Leeds Corpn., 1930; dep. town clk., Gloucs., 1935; Leamington Spa, 1939; loc. govt. bound. comsn., 1946; dep. comsn., 1950; advr., loc. govt., municipal, G.C., 1951; loc. govt. sec. and advr., 1953; perm. sec., 1954.

GREENWOOD, G. M.—b. 1908; ed. Rugby and Christ's Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1940-45; capt. (p.o.w., 1941-45); admin. offr., Uga., 1931; asst. dist. offr., Pal., 1935; admin. offr., Cyp., 1935; dist. offr., Uga., 1947; asst. ch. sec., 1948; estab. sec., 1950; admin. sec., 1952.

GREGG, B. L.—b. 1905; ed. Point Chev. Public Sch., N.Z.; Auckland Gram. Sch. and Auckland Univ. Coll., N.Z.; clk., registr-gen's dept., Fiji, 1929; clk. to atty.-gen., 1930; leg. distmnr., 1938; dep. registr-gen. and dep. registr., sup. ct., 1938; registr-gen., Fiji, 1950.

GREGG, J. R., Q.C. (Uga.).—b. 1899; ed. Royal Belfast Acad. Inst. and Harvard and Camb. Univs.; barrister-at-law, King's Inn, Dublin, Belfast, and Gray's Inn; police mag., St. V., 1929; atty.-gen., 1930; mag., G.C., 1933; cr. coun., Nyasa, 1936; solr.-gen., Uga., 1939; atty.-gen., 1943; puisne judge, Nig., 1948; H.K., 1953; govt. repr. on St. V. arrowroot bd., 1950; chmn., Uga. war transport comsn.

GREGG, W. D.—b. 1912; ed. Wolverhampton Gram. Sch. and St. Catharine's Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1940-45, capt.; senr. admin. asst., B'ham local educ. authy.; dir., educ., Sey., 1949; inspr., educ., Uga., 1953.

GREGORY, E. J.—b. 1907; ed. Durham Sch. and Wye Coll., post-grad. training, Queen's Coll., Oxford, and I.C.T.A.; man., St. Augustine exp. stn., Trin., 1933; agric. offr., Uga., 1938; senr. agric. offr., J'ca, 1946; dep. dir., agric. (res.), 1951; author of various publs. on citrus experiments and sugar cane variety trials, Trin.

GREGORY, G. B.—b. 1910; ed. King Edward VI Gram. Sch., Guildford, and Lodge Sch., Barb.; agric. asst., St. Kitts, 1931; St. L., 1936; agric. supt., Grenada, 1946; senr. agric. offr., B. Hond., 1949.

GREGORY, J. W.—b. 1894; ed. Eastwood and Beauvale Schs.; mil. serv., 1914-19; off. supt., comm. and ind., Pal., 1920; off. surveyor, cust. excise and trade, 1924; asst. collr., cust. and excise, 1926; collr., 1940; asst. dir., 1946; price contrlr., 1946; compt., cust. and excise, B. GREIG, J. L.—b. 1902; ed. Aberdeen, Brasenose Coll., Oxford, and I.C.T.A.; mil. serv., 1941-45; agric. offr., Mal., 1928; senr., 1946; dir., agric., N. Borneo, 1951.

GRELL, E.H.A.—b. 1916; ed. Dominica Gram. Sch.; mil. serv., 1943-46, maj.; asst. mstr., Gram. Sch., Montserrat, 1934, asst. supt. police, Leeward Is., 1947; labr. comsnr., St. Kitts, 1948.
GRELL, G. H. L.—b. 1915; ed. Dominica Gram. Sch.; clk., govt. depts., 1935-42; 1st asst. master, Gram. Sch., Montserrat, 1943-46; compt., inc. tax, Dominica, 1950.

GREY, R. F. A., O.B.E.—b. 1910; ed. Wellington Coll., Auckland Univ. Coll., N.Z., and Pembroke Coll., Camb.; barrister and solr., sup. ct., N.Z.; admin. offr., Nig., 1937; asst. fin. sec., 1948; cl. I, 1951; devpt. sec., 1952; sec. to gov.-gen. and coun. of ministers, 1954.

GRIFFIN, J. B., Q.C.—b. 1903; ed. Clongowes, Dublin and Camb. GRIFFIN, T. F.—b. 1906; ed. Birkenhead School and Liverpool Univ.; war serv., 1939-46, group capt.; apptd. col. serv., 1946; senr. marketing offr., Nig., 1950; dep. dir. (industries), 1951.

GRIFFITH-JONES, E. N., Q.C.—b. 1913; ed. Cheltenham Coll.; barrister-at-law, Gray's Inn, 1934; mag., S.S., 1939; mil. serv., 1941-46; cr. coun., Mal., 1946; senr. fedl. coun., 1948; solr.-gen., Ken., 1952.

GRIFFITH-WILLIAMS, G. C.—b. 1893; ed. Bromsgrove Sch. and Emmanuel Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law, Middle Temple; mil. serv., 1914-19; ch. just., St. V., 1935; puisne judge, Cyp., 1938.

GRIFFITHS, J. E. S., M.B.E.—b. 1908; ed. S. African Coll. Sch., Univ. of Cape Town and Selwyn Coll., Camb.; cadet, Tang., 1931; asst. dist. offr., 1933; dist. offr., 1943; senr., 1950; prov. comsnr., 1954; sleeping sickness work 1933 and 1935; co-ord. offr., Malya Sukumaland, 1950.

GRIFFITHS, J. F.—b. 1913; ed. St. Andrew's Coll., S.A.; mil. serv., 1940-45, W.O.I.; compt., stores, Basuto., 1949; asst. acctnt., Nyasa., 1950; acctnt., 1952; dep. acctnt.-gen., Fiji, 1953.

GRIFFITHS, W. G.—b. 1899; ed. Narberth County Sch.; mil. serv., 1941-46, capt. (p.o.w., 1942-45); Br. P.O., 1916; supt., P. and T., Mal., 1930; asst. contrlr., posts, 1938; p.a. to ch. censor., 1939; contrlr., posts, 1948; dir., 1952.

GRINSTED, W. A.—b. 1904; ed. GRIPPER, G. F.—b. 1906; ed. Marlborough, and Camborne Sch. of Metallic-ferous Mining; mil. serv., 1941-46; inspr., mines, F.M.S., 1930; asst. warden, mines, 1937; warden, Johore, 1946; dep. ch. inspr., Mal., 1950; ch. inspr., 1952.

HENNESSEY, R. S. F., C.M.G. (1954).—b. 1905; ed. Int. Sch., Newry, and St. Andrew's Coll., Dublin, Dublin and Lond. Univs.; pathologist, Uga., 1929; D.M.S. (Labs.), Pal., 1944; D.M.S., 1947; asst. med. advr., C.O., 1947; D.M.S., Uga., 1949; author of various papers on pathological subjects.

HENRIQUES, C. G. X.—b. 1908; ed. St. Francis Xavier's Coll., Lond., and Univ. Coll., Lond.; barrister-at-law; cr. coun., J'ca, 1939; res. mag., 1944; atty.-gen., B. Hond., 1950; mem., Island prob. comtee., J'ca, and prov. juvenile authy., J'ca.

HENRY, Sir James, Bt., M.C.—b. 1911; ed. Downside and University Coll., Lond.; barrister-at-law, Inner Temple; mil. serv., 1939-45; cr. coun., Tang., 1946; leg. dftsman., 1949; solr.-gen., 1952; jt. comsnr., revised edn. of laws of Tang., 1947-49.

HENRY, O. E.—b. 1916; ed. Gram. Sch., Antigua; asst. clk., Antigua, 1936; junr. clk., 1942; senr. clk., 1944; field asst., 1949; admin. sec., 1950; asst. admin. sec., 1952; treas., Montserrat, 1953.

HENVILLE, C. F.—b. 1912; ed. West Buckland Sch., N. Devon; barrister-at-law, Lincoln's Inn; clk., treas. dept., St. Kitts-Nevis, 1934; registr., sup. ct., 1943; cr. atty., 1944; atty.-gen., Wind. Is., 1950.

HEPBURN, B. A. St. J.—b. 1911; ed. Cornwall Coll., P'ca; secretariat, J'ca, 1930; asst. sec., 1944; prin. asst. sec., Sarawak, 1947; dev. sec., 1951.
HERCHENRODER, Sir Francis, Kt. Bach. (1953), Q.C. (1943).—b. 1896; ed. Royal Coll., Maur., and Middle Temple; barrister-at-law; dist. mag., Maur., 1923; addl. substitute procureur and adv.-gen., 1934; substitute proc. and adv.-gen., 1938; puisne judge, sup. ct., 1944; proc. and adv.-gen., 1945; ch. just., 1949; chmn., meat and milk comsn., 1942-43.

HERCHENRODER, M. V. M.—b. 1901; ed. Royal Coll., Maur., and King's Coll., Univ. of Lond.; computer, Royal Alfred Observatory, Maur., 1921; ast. dir., 1930; dir. of stats., 1945; author of various studies on met. subjects in Mauritius.

HERKLOTS, G. A. C.—b. 1902; ed. Trent Coll., Derbyshire, Leeds Univ. and Trinity Hall, Camb.; sec. for development, H.K., 1946; sec. for col. agric. res. and sec. comtece, for col. agric., animal health and for. res., C.O., 1948; prin., I.C.T.A., 1953; economic botanist to nutrition survey of Nyasal., 1939; editor, Hong Kong Naturalist, 1930-41, and of Journal of Hong Kong Fisheries Research Station; author of books on trees, flowers, fishes, birds and vegetables.

HEUSTIS, A. A., M.B.E.—b. 1911; ed. St. Mary's Sch., Belize; clk., survey dept., B. Hond., 1926; inc. tax dept., J'ca (secon.), 1945; 1st cl. clk., inc. tax dept., B. Hond., 1945; assessor, 1946.

HEWITT-MYRING, P.—b. 1900; ed. Eton; Gren. Gds., 1918-20; min. of inf., 1941-45; head, press sec., Br. inf. serv., New York, 1942-45; public rel. advr. to Br. co-chmn., Carib. Comsn., 1945; D.W.W.I., 1950.

HEWSON, M. G.—b. 1912; ed. Repton, St. Columba's and Dublin Univ.; ast. dist. comsnr., G.C., 1938; dist. comsnr., 1943; registr., co-op. soc., 1948; admin. offr., cl. I, 1952.

HEYLIGER, E. A.—b. 1916; ed. King's Coll., Lond.; barrister-at-law, Gray's Inn; registr., St. L., 1944; dist. offr., 1945; dist. mag., Grenada, 1948; cr. atty., St. L., 1954.

HEYS, H.—b. 1911; ed. Blackpool High Sch.; ch. acctnt., Cyp., 1945; acctnt. gen., Som., 1949; acctnt. gen., Nyasa., 1952.

HEYWOOD, G. S. P.—b. 1903; ed. Winchester Coll. and New Coll., Oxford; p.o.w., 1941-45; prof. ast., Royal Observatory, H.K., 1932; dir., 1946; mem., conf. of dir., inter. meteor. organ.; chmn., conf. on storm warning procedures, Manila, 1949.

HICKINBOTHAM, Maj. Sir Tom, K.C.M.G. (1953), K.C.V.O. (1954), C.M.G. (1951), C.I.E. (1944), O.B.E. (1939).—b. 1899; ed. R.M.C., Sandhurst; Indian Army, 1923-24 (N.W. Front. medal), 1924; Indian polit. serv., 1930; various appts., Aden, 1931-32, 1933-35 and 1938-39; polit. agent, Bahrain, 1937 and 1943-45; Muscat, 1939-41; Kuwait, 1941-43; Kalat, 1945-47; chmn., Port Trust and o/c trade registrn. dept., Aden, 1948; gov. and c-in-c., 1951.

HICKLING, C. F., C.M.G. (1951).—b. 1902; ed. Taunton Sch., and St. Catharine's Coll., Camb.; apptd. min. of agric. and fisheries, 1927; fisheries advr. to S. of S., Cols., 1945; author of numerous papers and monographs on fisheries, fisheries biology and zoology.

HICKLING, R. H.—b. 1920; ed. Buxton Coll. and Notts. Univ.; solr.; nav. serv., 1941; ast. atty.-gen., Sarawak, 1950; chmn., soc. welf. coun., 1952.

HICKS, A. H., C.B.E. (1950).—b. 1894; ed. Cardiff High Sch.; mil. serv., 1914-19, lt.; entd. C.A. off., 1913; secon. to G.C. as sec., harbr. dept., 1925-28; ast. head, pay dept., C.A., 1930; appts. dept., 1930; fin. dept., 1932; dep. head, stores dept., 1935; hd., 1939; prin. priority offr. to S. of S., Cols., 1944-48; hd. fin. dept., 1945; sec., E.A. Currency Bd., 1938-39.

HICKS, P. H.—b. 1916; ed. St. Paul's Sch. and Lond. Univ.; mil. serv., 1940-43, capt.; cadet engr., K.U.R. & H., 1937; ast. engr., 1940; section engr. (construct.), 1948; dist. engr., E.A.R. & H., 1950; senr., 1952; construct. engr., harbour dev., 1953; construct. engr., 1953; author of paper on Portal Peaks of Ruwenzori, R.G.S.

HIGGS, W. E.—b. 1915; ed. Public Sch., Harbour Is.; clk., medical dept., Bah., 1934; audit, 1953; ast. lab. offr., 1954.

HIGHAM, J. D., Order of St. Olaf (Chevalier 1st cl.) (1947).—b. 1914; ed. Manchester Gram. Sch. and Gonville and Caius Coll., Camb.; apptd. after compet. exam. ast. prin., admiralty, 1936; ast. p.s. to first lord, 1939; p.s. to parl. sec. and parl. clk., 1940; ag. prin., 1941; prin., C.O., 1946; ast. sec., 1948; secon. as under sec., S'pore, 1953.

HILL, C. de N.—b. 1917; ed. Cheltenham Coll. and St. Edmund Hall, Oxford; admin. offr., Nig., 1939; sec. for fin., Tang., 1954.

HILL, J. F. R., C.M.G. (1955).—b. 1905; ed. Marlborough and Lincoln Coll., Oxford; cadet, Tang., 1928; ast. dist. offr., 1930; dist. offr., 1940; dep. prov. comsnr., 1947; prov. comsnr., 1948; senr. prov. comsnr., 1952; mem., comms., wks. and dev. planning, 1952.

HILL, K. A. L.—b. 1899; ed. Dulwich Coll. and Camb. Univ.; mil. serv., 1918-19, 2nd lieut.; I.C.S., ast. mag., 1923; joint mag., 1924; ast. settlement offr., 1924; settlement offr., 1926; dist. mag., 1936; sec. to govt. of Bengal, 1940; registr., co-op. socs., S.L., 1948.

Pac. supply confce., Sydney, 1943; Wellington, 1945; mem. mining bd. and copra bd., 1946; chmn., comtec. for civ. serv. bldg. loans, 1946; fin. sec., Maur., 1952.

HINDLE, T.—b. 1915; ed. Blackburn Gram. Sch. and King's Coll., Camb.; cadet, G.C., 1938; asst. dist. comsnr., 1939; dist. comsnr., 1945; secon. labr. dept., 1946; senr. dist. comsnr., 1951; prin. asst. sec., 1954.

HINDS, T. S., O.B.E.—b. 1900; ed. Oratory Sch.; customs asst., Nyasa., 1927; dep. compt., customs, and excise comsnr., Maur., 1937; compt. of customs, harbourmr. and granary supt., 1946; M.L.C.; compt. of customs, Nyasa., 1947; contrlr., imp., exp. and prices, 1953; chmn., select comte. of leg. co. to report on rum, etc., Maur., 1947.

HIRST, H.—b. 1907; ed. Leeds, Glasgow, Cambridge Univs. and Univ. of Minnesota, U.S.A.; advr., animal husbandry, Malta, 1938; asst. dir., agric., Cyp., 1945; secon. F.A.O. of U.N., 1951-53 for duty, advr. animal husbandry, Ceylon.

HIRST, H. R.—b. 1903; ed. Cranleigh; asst. treas., Ken., 1925; senr. asst. treas., 1934; ag. fin. offr., Aden (secon.), 1939; asst. acctnt. gen., 1944; acctnt. gen., H.K., 1945; Nig., 1950.

HITCHIN, P. M.—b. 1913; ed. Colet Ct., St. Paul's & Law Soc. Sch. of Law; mil. serv., 1939-45; solr. (Eng.), col. leg. serv., R.A.R.O.; col. serv., 1946; registr. of high ct., 1946; registr.-gen., Nyasa., 1948.

HOBBES, R. E. T.—b. 1907; ed. Leighton Park Sch., Reading Univ.; agric. offr., Ken., 1932; senr., 1945; dep. dir., agric., 1950.

HOBBES, W. F. J.—b. 1908; ed. elem. sch. and Camberwell Sch. of Arts and Crafts; mil. serv., 1940-44; machinist-compositor, N. Rhod., 1932; asst. govt. printer, 1944; govt. printer, 1951.

HOBDAY, J. H. N., O.B.E.—b. 1907; ed. City of London Sch., Lond. Univ. and R. Vet. Coll.; appt., 1933; ch. vet. offr., Bech., 1935; D.V.S., N. Rhod., 1943; chmn., cattle marketing and control bd., and livestock impr. bd., N. Rhod.; mem., cold storage cont. and butter impr. bds., N. Rhod.

HOCHOY, S., O.B.E.—b. 1905; ed. St. Mary's Coll., Trin.; clk., port and marine dept., Trin., 1931; labr. offr., 1944; dep. indus. advr., 1946; comsnr. of labr., 1949.

HOCKENHULL, A. J. W.—b. 1915; ed. HOCKIN, V. T.—b. 1903; ed. Clarence Sch., Weston-super-Mare, and Sch. of Metall. Mining, Cornwall; inspr., mines, Tang., 1928; senr., 1936; ch. inspr., 1945; comsnt., mines., 1952.

HODGES, A. W.—b. 1907; ed. Sydney High Sch.; Queensland State public service, 1922; Australian C'wealth civil service, 1942; asst. compt., inc. tax., Mal., 1948; senr. asst. compt., inland rev., Fed. of Mal., 1952; compt., 1952.

HODGES, C. W., M.B.E. (1948).—b. 1911; asst. audr., S.L., 1934; C.A.D., 1938; Malta (secon.), 1942; C.A.D., 1943; audr. (later prin. audr.), Windward Is., 1945; dir., audit., Trin., 1949.

HODGSON, G. C. D., M.B.E. (1950).—b. 1913; ed. Diocesan Coll., Rondebosch, and Rhodes Univ., S.A.; cadet, Nyasa., 1939; mil. serv., 1940-42, lieut.; asst. dist. offr., Nyasa., 1942; prov. comsnr., 1952.

HODIWALLA, N. M., O.B.E.—b. 1900; ed. Univ. of Bombay; med. offr. of health, Aden Settlement, 1930; supt., prison, in addn., 1948-54.

HOGAN, M. J. P., C.M.G. (1953), Q.C.—b. 1908; ed. HOLDER, F. W., C.M.G. (1955), Q.C.—b. 1897; ed. Harrison Coll. and Codrington Coll., Barb. and Durham Univ.; barrister-at-law, Middle Temple; solr.-gen., Barb., 1936; judge, asst. ct. of appeal, 1942; solr.-gen., B. Guiana, 1944; atty.-gen., 1946.

HOLLAND, W. J. G.—b. 1902; ed. Ripley Convt. Sch., Surrey, Bedford Sch. and R.M.C., Sandhurst; hd. constable, Fiji, 1926; sub-inspr., 1926; asst. supt., police, 1942; supt., 1943; dep. comsnr., 1947.

HOLMAN, Lt.-Col. H. J.—b. 1906; ed. Alleyn's, Dulwich, Royal Coll., Sci., and Imp. Coll., Sci. and Tech.; mil. serv., 1939-43, lt.-col.; Imp. Inst., 1928; sec., col. prod. res. coun. and p.a. to dir. of res., 1943; from 1947 also sec., col. insecticides, etc., comte.; from 1949 also sec., inter.-deptl. comte. on locust control; admin. and finance offr.
HOLTZ, N. F. de B.—b. 1903; ed. Wolmer's Boys' Sch.; entered Jca govt. serv., 1921; asst. treas., 1946; dep. acctnt.-gen., 1950; acctnt.-gen., 1953; spec. work in connexion with Mona reservoir scheme; mem. of comttee. appd. to revise colony's fin. instructions; mem. of anomalies comttee., 1950.

HONE, E. D., C.M.G. (1953), C.V.O. (1954), O.B.E.—b. 1911; ed. Wellington Coll., Rhodes Univ. HONE, Sir Ralph, K.C.M.G. (1951), K.B.E. (civ.), 1946), C.B.E. (mil.), 1943), K.M.C. (1918), T.D. (1946), K.S.J. (1950), Q.C. (Gib. and Uga.).—b. 1896; ed. Vardean, Brighton and Lond. S.E. circ., 1923; reg. hist., C.t., Zanz., 1925; res. mag., 1928; treas. coun., Tang., 1930; ag. asst. legal advr., C.O./D.O., 1933; atty.-gen., Gib., 1933; Uga., 1937; comdt., Uga., 1942; W.O., 1943-44; ch. civ. aff. offr., Mal., 1945; sec. gen. to gov. gen., Mal., 1946; dep. comsnr.-gen. (col. affrs.), S.E. Asia, 1948; gov. and c.in-c., N. Borneo, 1950; ret., re-app. asst. legal advr., C.O./D.O., 1954; author of Index to Laws of Gibraltar, 1933; rev'd ed. Laws of Gibraltar, 1933; Handbook of Native Courts, 1939.

HOOD, Lt.-Gen. Sir Alexander, G.B.E. (1946), K.C.B. (1943), K.C.V.O. (1953), C.B. (1942), C.B.E. (1939).—b. 1888; ed. Geo. Watson's Coll. and Edin. Univ.; mil. serv., 1912-41, lt.-gen.; hse. surg., roy. inf., Edin., 1910; dir.-gen., army med. serv.; gov. and c.in-c., Berm., 1949.

HOOTON, A., Q.C.—b. 1912; ed. Lincoln Sch. and Queen's Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law, Gray's Inn; mil. serv., 1939-45, mai.; cr. coun., Pal., 1946; H.K., 1948; solr. gen., 1952.

HOOTON, J. C., M.B.E. (Mil.), Croix de Guerre.—b. 1912; barrister-at-law; mil. serv., 1940-46, lt.-col. (desps.); Pal. police, 1932; asst. supt., police, G.C., 1937; senr. asst., 1946; supr., 1948; secon. cr. coun., 1950; cr. coun., 1951; senr. asst. sec., E.A.H.C., 1953.

HOPE-JONES, A.—b. 1911; ed. Kirkby Lonsdale Sch., Christ's Coll., Camb., and Columbia Univ., U.S.A.; mil. serv., 1939-45; econ. and commercial advr., Ken., 1946; sec., comrce. and industry, 1948; mem. HOPKINS, C. E., M.B.E.—b. 1904; ed. Uppingham C. of E. Sch.; warder, prisons, S.S., 1929; aux. fire serv., S'pore, 1941; asst. contrlr., immig., Fed. of Mal., 1947; dep. contrlr., 1950.

HOPKINS, I. M.—b. 1902; ed. Bellahouston Acad., Jordan Hill Coll. for Teachers, and Univ. of Glasgow; prtn., govt. training coll. for teachers, Trin., 1935; asst. dir., educ., 1947; dep. dir., 1950; supt. tech. educ. and vocational training centre for ex-service men; chmn. and mem. of educ. and industrial training comtess.; supt. of tech. classes; author of Vocational Education in Trinidad.

HOPKINSON, Henry, C.M.G. (1944), M.P. for Taunton Division of Somerset since 1950.—b. 1902; ed. Eton and Trinity Coll., Camb.; entered dipl. serv., 3rd sec., Washington, 1924; 2nd sec., F.O., 1929; Stockholm, 1931; asst. p.s. to S.of.S., foreign affrs., 1932; Cairo, 1934; 1st sec., 1936; Athens, 1938; war cab. sec't, 1939; p.s. to P.U.S. of S., foreign affrs., 1940; ag. counsellor of embassy, office of min. of state, M.E., 1941; min. plenipotentiary, Lisbon, 1943; dep. Br. high comsnr. in Italy (with rank of min.), 1944-46; resigned to enter politics, 1946; head of Conservative parly. sec't, 1946-50; sec', o'seas trade, 1951; min. of state, col. affrs., 1952.

HORNER, A. W.—b. 1909; ed. Felsted Sch.; mil. serv., 1939-46, lt.-col.; asst. sec., Ken., 1950; col. admin. serv., 1953; asst. comsnr., lands, 1953.

HORNER, O. J. W., M.B.E.—b. 1899; cler. offr., Sey., 1919; clk. to gov. and clk. exec. and leg. couns., 1932; postmstr., 1939.

HOWE, Sir Gerard, Kt. Bach. (1939), Q.C.—b. 1899; ed. Armagh Royal Sch. and Trinity Coll., Dublin; barrister-at-law (of H.M. coun.); mil. serv., 1916–20, maj.; res. mag., Ken., 1930; cr. coun., G.C., 1934; S.S., 1937; solr.-gen., Nig., 1941; atty.-gen., 1946; ch. just., H.K., 1950.

LAWSON, D. H.—b. 1903; ed. The Wells Hse., Epsom Coll. and Pembroke Coll., Camb.; apptd., Nig., 1928; exec. engr., 1937; senr., 1945; D.D.P.W., Ken., 1949; D.P.W., W. regn., Nig., 1952.

LAYCOCK, Sir Robert, K.C.M.G. (1954), C.B. (1943), D.S.O. (1942)—b. 1907; ed. Eton and R.M.C., Sandhurst; Major-General (retd.) late Royal Horse Guards, served 1939-45 in M.E., Sicily, Italy and N.W. Europe; chief of combined ops., 1943-47; gov. and c-in-c., Malta, 1954.

LAZARD, R. A., J.—b. 1913; ed. Dulwich Coll. and Univ. Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1940-43, capt.; asst. treas., Tang., 1936; senr. acctnt., 1947; dep. acctnt.-gen., 1949; acctnt.-gen., S.L., 1952.

LEACH, E. W.—b. 1906; ed. Eastbourne Gram. Sch., Reading Univ., post-grad. courses at Oxford and I.C.T.A.; supt. of agric., Nig., 1928; senr. agric. offr., 1939; senr. agric. supt., Gam., 1939; dep. dir., agric. (crop husbandry), Trin., 1945; dir., agric., 1948; G.C., 1952.

LEE, F. B.—b. 1917; ed. Itchen Sch., S'ton; mil. serv., 1939-46, capt.; acctnt.-gen., 1952; E.A.H.C., 1953.

LEECHMAN, B., C.M.G. (1952), O.B.E. (1941)—b. 1901; ed. Oundle Sch.; cadet, Tang., 1925; asst. dist. offr., 1928; dist. offr., 1937; dep. prov. comsnr., 1944; labr. comsnr., 1946; mem. for labr., educ. and soc. welf., 1948; mem. for soc. serv., 1948; sec., E.A. econ. coun., Nairobi, 1940-41; dir., econ. cont., Aden, 1943-45; sec., maj. wks. comtce., 1937; sec., cent. dev. comtce., 1939-40; dep. food and export contr., 1939-40; sec., E.A. del., E. group confce., 1940; E.A. del. to U.S.A. and Rhod.; war prod. and supply liaison, 1941; mem. econ. cont. bd., 1942-43; i/c. rubber prod., 1942-43; dep. chmn. and exec. offr., econ. cont. bd., 1945-46.

LEEMBRUGGEN, C. E., O.B.E. (1954).—b. 1899; ed. Richmond Coll. and St. Thomas' Coll., Ceylon; mil. serv., 1918-19; customs dept., Fiji, 1920; collr., customs, 1940; collr. customs and excise, Nig., 1942; asst. compt., customs, S.L., 1945; prin. collr., customs and excise, Nig., 1949; asst. compt., 1951; dep., 1951.

LEEMING, R. A.—b. 1905; ed. Alleyn's Sch. and Lond Univ.; suptg. civ. engrn., Admty., 1938-46; D.D.P.W. (drainage, irrig. and sea def.), B. Guiana, 1950; dir., B. Guiana rice dev. co., 1953; dir., drainage and irrig., 1954.

LEES, E. C. L.—b. 1901; ed. Gidea Park Coll. and Warwick Sch.; mil. serv., 1940-41, capt.; staff survr., Tang., 1927; senr., 1939; dep. dir., surveys, Uga., 1950.

LE MARE, D. W.—b. 1912; ed. Manchester Gram. Sch., Brasenose and St. John's Colls., Oxford; lect. and demonsr., Oxford Univ., 1935-37; mil. serv., 1942-46, capt. (desps.); asst. to dir., fisheries, S.S. & F.M.S., 1938; dir., fisheries, Mal. and S'pore, 1947; U.K. del. Indo.-Pac. fisheries coun., Sydney, 1950; S. Pac. fisheries conf., Noumea, 1952; chmn., sci. advsy. comtce. on animal and veg. resources.

LEMMON, R. C.—b. 1906; ed. St. Olave's Sch., Lond.; treas. acctnt., Mal., 1929; senr., 1947; acctnt.-gen., H.K., 1950.

LEMON, R. M. L.—b. 1915; ed. Shardlow Hall Sch., Malvern Coll. and Balliol Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1940-46, lt.-col.; admin. asst., E.A.R. & H., 1950; ch. estab. offr., 1952.

LENNOX-BOYD, Rt. Hon. Alan T., P.C., M.P.—b. 1904; ed. Sherborne and Ch. Ch., Oxford; pres., Oxford Union, 1926; M.P.(C.) for mid-Bedfordshire since 1931; lieut., R.N.V.R., 1940-43; partly. sec., min. of labour, 1938-39; home security, 1939; food, 1939-40; aircraft prod., 1943-45; min. of state, colonial affrs., 1951-52; minister of transport and civ. aviation, 1952; S. of S., colonies, 1954; visited Malaya, Br. Caribbean and E. African terrs.

LESLIE, E. J. A.—b. 1912; ed. Uppingham and Sidney Sussex Coll., Camb.; cadet, Ken., 1934; registr. of co-op. soc., 1948; admin. sec., 1953; dist. comsnr., 1953.
LESLEIE, W. S.—b. 1900; ed. Wesley High Sch.; apptd. B. Hond., 1918; 1st cl. clk., 1926; senr. cust. offr., 1940; acctnt., P.W.D., 1944; dist. comsnr., 1951.

LETCHWORTH, T. E.—b. 1906; ed. Downside and Christ's Coll., Camb.; cadet, Nig., 1928; secon., Gam., 1943-45; admin. offr., cl. II, 1948; cl. I, 1954.

LEVACK, G. M.—b. 1918; ed. Edin. Acad. and Balliol Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1940-43; asst. dist. comsnr., G.C., 1940; secon. Pal., 1947-48; dist. comsnr., 1949; admin. offr., cl. II, 1951; cl. I, 1954.

LEVERETT, C. W., M.B.E. (Mil.)—b. 1907; ed. Harpley Sch. and St. Mary's Sch., Gt. Massingham, Norfolk; mil. serv., 1940-45, lt.-col.; clk., T.R. & P.S., 1931; asst. traff. supt., 1945; dist. traff. supt., Pal., 1946; E.A.R. & H., 1948; asst. supt. and regl. asst., 1952; traff. supt. and regl. offr., 1952; regl. rep. (Tang.), 1954.

LEVERSEDGE, L. F.—ed. St. Paul's Sch., Darjeeling, India, St. Peter's Sch., York, and St. John's Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law, Inner Temple; cadet, N. Rhod., 1926; dist. offr., 1928; prov. comsnr., 1947; senr., 1949; dev. sec., 1951; mem. N. Rhod. police comsn. of enquiry, 1946-47.

LEVY, N. C. A.—b. 1912; ed. Merchant Taylors' Sch., Gt. Crosby; asst. acctnt., Nig. rlwy., 1936; admin. asst., 1943; gr. I, prin. asst., gen. man., 1946; asst. civ. serv. comsnr., 1949; banking and exch. cont. offr., 1953; chmn., N. reg. mktg. bds., 1954.

LEWEY, Sir Arthur Werner, Kt. Bach. (1954), Q.C. (Jca.)—b. 1894; ed. St. Paul's and Trinity Hall, Camb.; barrister-at-law, Inner Temple, 1920; mil. serv., 1914-19; police mag., Gam., 1930; rec. coun., Ken., 1932; solr.-gen., Uga., 1936; atty.-gen., J'ca., 1939; G.C., 1943; leg. advr., to res. min., W.A., 1944; and to W.A.C., 1946; just. of appeal, W.A. ct. of appeal, 1948; ch. just., N. Rhod., 1952.

LEWIS, B.—b. 1912; ed. St. Mark's Sch., Kensington, and R.C.M.; mil. serv., 1940-46, maj.; sec., E.A. prod. and supp. council, 1946; dep. dir., trade, transport and industry, N. Rhod., 1951; dir., 1953; comsnr., trade, Aden, 1954.

LEWIS, E. A.—b. 1900; ed. Univ. Coll. of Wales, Aberystwyth; mil. serv., R.A.F., 1917-18; vet. entom., Ken., 1930; ch. field zoologist, 1946; dir., E.A. tsese reclam. dept., E.A.H.C., 1949; dir., E.A.T.T.R. and R.O., 1952; author of numerous publs. on helminths, ticks and tick-borne diseases, tsese and trypan.

LEWIS, I. E. G.—b. 1904; ed. Malvern Coll.; barrister-at-law; regist., high ct., Zanz., 1937; res. mag., 1941; Uga., 1941; puisne judge, 1954.

LEWIS, J. H., O.B.E. (1955).—b. 1904; ed. Plumtree Sch., S. Rhod., Rhodes Univ., S.A., and Cambridge; cadet, Ken., 1931; senr. dist. comsnr., 1951; comsnr., prisons, 1952.

LEWIS, P. C.—b. 1912; ed. Inter. Sch. and Gram. Sch., St. V.; barrister-at-law, Middle Temple; regist. and addl. mag., St. L., 1940; ch. censor, 1940-43; mag. and inc. tax comsnr., Dominica, 1943; cr. atty., St. V., 1945; St. L., 1952; atty.-gen., Leeward Is., 1954.

LEWIS, T. D.—b. 1911; mil. serv., 1939-48, lt.-col.; D.P.W., Somalia, 1942; and res. areas, Ethiopia, 1946-48; asst. town engnr., Kumasi, 1935; exec. engnr., P.W.D., G.C., 1938; gr. I, 1946; asst. D.P.W., 1949; D.D.P.W., 1952; author of Somalia Handbook.

LEWIS-JONES, W. W.—b. 1909; ed. Cotham Sch. and Downing Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1939-44, sqdn. ldr.; supt., educ., Tang., 1930; asst. mstr., Queen's Coll., B. Guiana, 1931; a.d.c. and p.s. to o.a.g., 1934; educ. offr., Ken., 1935; prin., 1949; senr., 1950; dir., educ., Fiji, 1953; mem., B. Guiana air bd. and of Fiji b'casting comsn.

LEWTHWAITE, R., C.M.G. (1955), O.B.E. (1945)—b. 1894; ed. Kendal Gram. Sch., Magdalen Coll., Oxford, and Middlesex Hosp. Med. Sch., Lond.; mil. serv., 1916-19, lieut., 1944-45, field director, med. res. coun., scrub typhus res. unit, attd. H.Q., S.E.A.C.; res. student in trop. med., inst. for med. res., K. Lumpur, 1926; senr. pathologist, 1931; dir., 1945; dir., col. med. res., 1949; N. Persian forces mem. medal for res. in trop. med., 1936; author of various pp. in med. lit. on typhus fevers, rabies, etc.

LEYDEN, J. L., C.B.E. (1948), O.B.E. (1945).—b. 1904; ed. Durham Univ.; war serv., 1942-45, lt.-col.; asst. res., Burma, 1927; dir., front. areas admin., 1946; econ. liaison offr., E. and Cent. Afr. reg., C.O., 1948; liaison offr., E. and Cent. Afr., C.O., 1950; sec. to various comtees. under Burma govt.; dir., Uga. Development Corp., Consolidated Properties Ltd., and Tororo Exploration Co. Ltd.; author of History of Hagaw Hill Tracts, 1936, and Kachins of the Hukawng Valley, 1941, 1943, 1944.

LIESCHING, Sir Percivale, G.C.M.G. (1951), K.C.B. (1947), K.C.V.O. (1953), K.C.M.G. (1944), C.M.G. (1932)—b. 1895; ed. Bedford Sch. and Brasenose Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1914-18, capt. (desps.); asst. prin., C.O., 1920; D.O., p.s. to P.U.S. of S., 1925; prin., 1927; accom. U.K. del. to L. of N. Assembly, 1927, and to sig. of Pact of Paris, 1928; first sec., office of U.K. high comsnr., Canada, 1928; polit. sec., Pretoria, 1933; official sec., Canberra, 1936; asst. U.S. of S., D.O., 1939; sec. B. of T. as second sec., 1942; led dels. to Washington to discuss commercial policy, 1943 and 1945; secon. M. of Food as perm. sec., 1946; led mission to Ottawa, 1947; P.U.S. of S., C.R.O., 1949; high comsnr. for Basuto., Bech. Prot. and Swaz., 1955.

LILLEY, R.—b. 1906; ed. Bedford Sch. and Pembroke Coll., Oxford; asst. audr., Tang., 1930; S.S. and F.M.S., 1936; senr. asst. audr., Nig., 1938; audr. (later dir., aud.), B. Guiana, 1945; senr. audr., Uga., 1948; prin., 1949.
LILICRUP, E. H. C.—b. 1898; ed. Plymouth Coll. and Jesus Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1914-18; asst. audr., G.C., 1923; audr., central off., 1928; asst. audr., Nig., 1929; senr. asst., 1939; prin. asst., 1946; dep. dir. aud., Mal., 1947; ret. 1953; D.O., Cocos Is. (contract), 1954.

LINDO, H. L.—b. 1911; ed. J'ca Coll. and Keble Coll., Oxford; inspr., schs., J'ca, 1935; prin. clk., secretariat, 1943; asst. sec., 1945; prin. asst. sec., 1950; adminr., Dominica, 1952.

LINES, G. W., C.B.E. (1955), M.B.E.—b. 1902; ed. Camb. and Cty. High Sch. and St. Catharine's Coll., Camb.; supt., agric., Nig., 1925-30 (resig.); agric. offr., Nig., 1935; senr., S.L., 1944; prin. agric. offr., 1945; dep. dir., agric., 1949; dir. agric., 1951. (Deceased 11.2.55.)

LINGLEY, L. G.—b. 1910; ed. Plymouth Coll. and Downing Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law, Inner Temple; dist. mag., G.C., 1937; puisne judge, 1951.

LINTON, R. D.—b. 1904; ed. Mountjoy Sch., Dublin, Queen's Univ., Belfast, Oxford Univ. and I.C.T.A.; agric. offr., Tang., 1929; senr. agric. offr., 1944; dep. dir., agric., G.C., 1949.

LISTON, Right Rev. D.—b. 1900; ed. Blackrock Coll., Dublin, Nat. Univ., Ireland, and Gregorian Univ., Rome; 1st cl. priest, 1938; Bishop of Port Louis, Maur., 1949.

LISTON, J. M.—b. 1909; ed. Glasgow High Sch. and Univ.; med. offr., Ken., 1935; dir., med. and health serv., Sarawak, 1947; dep. dir., H.K., 1952; D.M.S. (desig.) Tang., 1955.

LITTLEJOHN, N. K.—b. 1900; ed. Robert Gordon's Coll., Aberdeen; mil. serv., 1941-45, capt.; engrg., P.W.D., H.K., 1926; exec. engrg., 1948; asst. dir., 1950; mem., dangerous goods comttee., 1949.

LITTLEWOOD, H. K.—b. 1908; ed. King's Coll., Taunton, and R. Vet. Coll., London; appd., 1940; senr. vet. offr., Nig., 1949; asst. D.V.S., later ch. vet. offr., W. regn., 1950.

LITTLEWOOD, V. H. K.—b. 1911; ed. Sevenoaks Sch., St. Catharine's Coll., Camb., and Oriel Coll., Oxford; cadet, G.C., 1935; dist. comsnr., 1942; supvr., col. serv. courses, Camb., 1947; admin. offr., Nyasa., 1949; ch. estab. offr., 1950.

LEWELLIN 1st Baron (cr. 1945), P.C., G.B.E. (1939), O.B.E. (1926), M.C., T.D.—b. 1893; ed. Eton and Univ. Coll., Oxford; barrister-at-law; mil. serv., 1914-19, col., M.P. (U) 1929-45; par. p.s., P.M.G., 1931; to 1st comsnr., wks., 1931; asst. govt. whip, 1935; civil lord, Admty., 1937; par. sec., min. supply, 1939; to min. aircraft prod., 1940; war trans., 1941; pres., bd. trade, 1942; min., aircraft prod., 1942; min. res. in Washington for supply, 1942; min., food, 1943-45; pres. fed. chambers of commerce, Br. Empire, 1948-51; mem., B.B.C. gen. advy. coun., 1952; gov.-gen., Fed. of Rhod. and Nyasa., 1953.

LLOYD, 2nd Baron (cr. 1925) of Dolobran M.B.E. (1945)—b. 1912; ed. Eton and Camb. Univ.; mil. serv., 1939-45, capt.; member of L.C.C., 1949-51; lord-in-waiting to Geo. VI and Eliz. II, 1951-52; joint U.S. of S. for Home Dep. with responsibility for Welsh affairs, Nov. 1952; Par. U.S. of S., Cols., Oct. 1954.

LLOYD, H. M.—b. 1907; ed. Christ's Coll., Finchley, Bishops Stortford Coll., and Royal Coll. of Sci., Lond.; entom. dept. of tsetse res., Tang., 1930; dir. of tsetse res., 1947; author Notes on the Bionomics of Glossina swynnertonii and The occurrence of G. Morsitans in Tanganyika on vegetational types other than the Isoberlinia-Brachystegia communities.

LLOYD, Sir Thomas, G.C.M.G. (1951), K.C.B. (1949), K.C.M.G. (1947), C.M.G. (1943).—b. 1896; ed. Rossall Sch. and Gonville and Caius Coll., Camb.; cadet, Royal Mil. Acad., 1915; 2nd lieut., R.E., 1916; served in Egypt and Pal. (desps.), 1917; apptd. under reconstruction scheme, asst. prin., min. of health, 1920; attd. to C.O. as asst. prin., 1921; ag. prin., 1927; p.s. to P.U.S. of S., 1929; prin., 1929; sec., comsn. on Pal. disturbances, 1929-30; sec., W.I. royal comsn., 1938-39; asst. sec., 1939; asst. U.S. of S., 1943; P.U.S. of S., Cols., 1947.

LLOYD-WILLIAMS, F. N.—b. 1908; ed. Brighton Coll. and Camb. Univ.; asst. dir., schs. b'casting, B.B.C., 1934; Nig. repve., Br. coun., 1943; dep. dir. (schs. b'casting) dept. of b'casting, Mal., 1946; dep. dir. of b'casting, 1950.

LOCH, A. J. D. C.—b. 1915; ed. Imp Service Coll., Windsor, and Edin. Univ.; mil. serv. and p.o.w., 1941-45; asst. prin., C.O., 1937; M.C.S., cl. V, 1940; asst. registr., companies, etc., S'pore, 1941; cl. IV, 1943; secon. C.O., 1946; asst. to econ. advr., M.U., 1946; cl. III, asst. econ. sec., 1947; D.O., Ulu Kelantan, 1948; sec. to mem. for agric. and forestry, 1951; cl. IC, 1952; ag. mem. for econ. affrs., 1954.

LOCKHART, R. H.—b. 1900; ed. Lycée Schoelcher, Martinique, and Xaverian Coll., Clapham; called to bar, Middle Temple; mag. and crown atty., Montserrat, 1949; cr. atty., Antigua, 1954.

LOCKIE, J. R.—b. 1904; ed. Campbelltown Sch. and Edin Univ.; apptd. forestry dept., B. Guiana, 1926; Nig., 1936; consvtr., 1947; asst. ch. consvtr., 1949; ch. consvtr., N. reg., 1951; consvtr, N. reg., 1954.

LOFTING, H. G.—b. 1909; apptd. N. Rhod., 1933; asst. govt. printer. Ken. 1951.

LOGAN, W. E. M.—b. 1911; ed. Cheltenham Coll. and Queen's Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1939-44, maj.; asst. consvr., forests, G.C., 1935; Ken., 1947; consvr., Tang., 1952; dep. ch. consvr., 1954; author of various pp. and articles on forestry and harvesting forest crops.
LONGFIELD, J. E., O.B.E.—b. 1908; ed. Bedford Sch., Camb. Univ. and Trinity Coll., Dublin, 1932; cl. III, 1936; cl. II, 1942; col. admin. serv., 1946; res., 1953.

LONGRIDGE, T. W.—b. 1908; ed. Royal Gram. Sch., Newcastle-upon-Tyne, and Durham Univ.; mil. serv., 1940-43; maj.; engr., geol. surv., Nig., 1934; asst. water engr., Cyp., 1938; water engr., N. Rhod., 1945; dir., water dev. and irrig., 1947; irrig. invest. in Aden in 1944.

LONSDALE, A.—b. 1917; ed. Oldham Hulme Gram. Sch.; called to Bar, Gray's Inn; mil. serv., 1940-46; maj.; cr. coun., H.K., 1945; legal dftsmn., G.C., 1930.

LOPEY, W. M., M.B.E. (1951).—b. 1894; ed. Codrington Coll., Barb.; asst. mstr., Gram. Sch., St. V., 1918; hdmstr., 1939.

LORD, G. E. L.—b. 1907; ed. Felsted Sch. and Christ's Coll., Camb.; asst. audr., G.C., 1930; Pal., 1935; jt. audr., Som. and Aden., 1939; audr. (later prin. audr.), Fiji and W. Pac., 1946; dep. dir. of audit (later dep. audr.-gen.), G.C., 1951.

LORD, L., O.B.E. (1951).—b. 1893; ed. Ashton-u-Lyne Sec. Sch., Ontario Agric. Coll. and Lincoln Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1914-18; lieut.; dep. dir., agric., Burma, 1920; univ. dem., sch. of rural econ., Oxford, 1925; econ. bot., dept. of agric., Ceylon, 1926; agric. offr., eastern div., 1930; o/c exper. stn., Peradeniya, and prin., sch. of trop. agric., 1935; sec., col. advsy. coun. of agric., animal health and forestry, 1941; asst. agric. advr., C.O., 1948.

LORD, W. T., I.S.O. (1948), J.P.—b. 1894; ed. Queen's Coll., B. Guiana, King's Coll., Lond. Univ. and Imp. Coll. of Sci. and Tech.; apptd. col. serv., 1911; govt. surv., lands and mines dept., B. Guiana, 1919; warden, mining dists., 1927; surv. and computer, Br. section, B. Guiana-Brazil boundary comsn., 1930-35; warden and travelling mag., mining dists., 1936; supt., surveys, lands and mines dept., 1941; dep. comsnr., lands and mines, 1949; comsnr., 1950; dir., land setl., 1954; chmn., cent. housing and plan. authy.; prof. and tech. appts. bd.; dir., widows and orphans pensions fund; mem., bd. of examiners for land survs.; comsnr., Georgetown planning comsnrs.

LOSONCZI, E.—b. 1912; Naples and London Univs.; mil. serv., 1942-47, capt. R.A.M.C.; dist. M.O., Dominica, 1947; M.O.H., B. Hond., 1953.

LOUDON, T. M.—b. 1910; ed. Harvey Gram. Sch. and Queen's Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1939-45; dist. offr., Ken., 1946; secon. C.O., Feb.-Nov., 1946; offr. i/c, E.A. hides, tanning and allied industries bureau, 1942, dir. produce disposal, 1953; E.A. and Ken. jute contr., 1953.

LOUSY, A. F. L.—b. 1916; ed. St. Mary's Coll., St. L.; called to bar, Middle Temple; local barrister, St. L., registr. and addl. mag., 1946; mag., Antigua, 1951; cr. atty. and mag., Montserrat, 1954.

LOVELACE, A., M.B.E., M.C.—b. 1907; ed. Dorchester Gram. Sch., Univ. Coll. of South-West, Exeter, and Birkbeck Coll., Univ. of Lond.; mil. serv., 1936-45, lt.-col.; educ. offr., Maur., 1946; civ. comsnr., 1949; admrn., Antigua, 1954.

LOVERIDGE, A. J., C.M.G. (1954), O.B.E. (1947).—b. 1904; ed. Emmanuel Sch. and St. John's Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law, Middle Temple; admin. cadet, G.C., 1930; asst. dist. comsnr., 1931; dist. comsnr., 1939; jud. advr., 1945; senr., 1947; ch. regional offr., 1951.

LOW, G. C.—b. 1902; ed. Charterhouse and Magdalen Coll., Oxford; barrister-at-law; mil. and nav. serv., 1940-46, sub-lieut.; res. mag., Uga., 1935; puisne judge, 1950.

LOW, T. B., I.S.O.—b. 1902; ed. Liverpool Inst.; mil. serv. and p.o.w., 1941-45; boarding offr., H.K., 1930; master of R.T. Kau Sing in addn., 1933; asst. shipping mstr., 1939; marine offr., 1948; senr. (port control), 1949, marine mag. in addn., 1950; chmn., pilotage bd. and port wel., comte., 1951; asst. dir., marine (port control), 1951; master attendant, S'pore, 1954.

LOWE, A. G.—b. 1901; ed. Auckland Gram. Sch. and Univ., N.Z.; barrister and solr. (N.Z.); war serv., 1939-45, sqdn. ldr.; leg. sec., govt. of Tonga, 1938-40; cr. coun., Ken., 1945; leg. sec., Malta, 1949; ag. lieut.-gov., periods 1949-53; judge, high ct., Tang., 1953.

LUCAS, A. J. R.—b. 1904; ed. Plymouth Coll.; asst. audr., Nig., 1927; Uga., 1937; senr. asst., 1944; prin. audr., Cyp., 1947; dir., audt., Uga., 1952.

LUCIE-SMITH, M. N.—b. 1911; ed. St. George's Coll., Weybridge, Wye Coll., Univ. of Lond., I.C.T.A., Redlands and Calif. Univs., U.S.A.; mil. serv., 1940-43, 2nd lt.; agric. offr., Trin., 1944; agronomist, 1947; asst. dir., agric., G.C., 1952; dir., Maur., 1954; reported on lime cultivation in Dominica (1945) and Jamaica (1951) for W.I. Limes Assoc.; various pubns. on orchard crops in tech. periodicals.

LUCKHAM, H. A. L.—b. 1904; ed. Blundell's Sch. and Balliol Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1944-46; cadet, F.M.S., 1928, 2nd mag., K. Lumpur, 1932; audr. and publ. prosecutor, Kelantan, 1933; sec., rubber reg., Johore, 1935; D.O., Tampin, 1938; secon., Ken., sec., dir., imports, E.A., 1942; asst. sec., Ken., sec't, 1943; dep. comsnr., Perak cent., 1946; D.O., Kinta, 1947, Klang, 1950; dep. sec., econ. affrs., S'pore, 1950; comsnr., lands, Mal., 1951.

LUGTON, W. G. M.—b. 1906; ed. Trinity Academy and Cathedral Choir Sch., Edinburgh, and Lond. Univ.; probn. offr., Lond. probn. serv., 1936; welf. offr., min. labr. and nat. surv., 1941; senr. welf. offr., Cyp., 1945; soc. welf. advr., N. Rhod., 1951; dir., soc. welf. and probn. serv., 1952.

LUKE, Sir Stephen, K.C.M.G. (1953), C.M.G. (1946).—b. 1905; ed. St. George's, Harpenden, and Wadham Coll., Oxford; apptd. after compet. exam. asst. inspr., taxes
LUKE, T. C., M.B.E. (1946).—b. 1910; ed. S.L. Gram. Sch. and St. Edmund Hall, Oxford; clerical serv., 1928; asst. col. sec., S.L., 1941; clk. of leg. coun., 1942-46; ch. asst. col. sec., 1950.

LUMSDEN, J. L.—b. 1914; ed. N. Berwick and Edin. Coll. of Art; mil. serv., 1939-45, lieut.; ch. architect, Maur., 1950-53.

Univ.; mil. serv., 1914-18; del. for S.L. and the Gam. to Pan African Health Conf., Jo'burg, 1935; M.O., Gam., 1924; S.L., 1931; S.M.O., G.C., 1933; Gam., 1935; asst. D.M.S., G.C., 1936; D.D.M.S., 1938; Nig., 1939; asst. med. advr., C.O., 1944; dep. med. advr., C.O., 1945; dep. chief med. offr., 1948.

RAE, N., C.B.E.—b. 1892; mil. serv., 1917-20, lt.; entd. C.A. off., 1911; asst. head of dept., 1919; dep. head, stores dept., 1932; dep. ch. clk., 1935; head, stores dept., 1945.

RAGG, H. A.—b. 1904; ed. in N.S.W. and Fiji; civ. engrn. apprentice, P.W.D., Fiji, 1923; junr. draughtsman, 1927; draughtsman, 1932; engrn. asst., 1936; asst. engrn., 1940; exec. engrn., 1943; D.D.P.W., 1952; D.P.W., 1954.

RAIKES, T. R. C.—b. 1911; ed. Central and Secondary Schs., Port Talbot, S. Wales; Br. P.O., 1928; survr., Nig., 1944; div. survr., 1949; asst. P.M.G., S.L., 1952; P.M.G., 1952.

RAINEY, R. C.—b. 1913; ed. Purbrook Park County High Sch., Hants., Imp. Coll. of Sci. and Tech., L.S.H.T.M.; mil. serv., 1940-46, maj.; research staff, Emp. Cotton Growing Corp., 1937-40; secon. E.A. antilocust directorate, 1947, and inter. red locust cont. serv., 1948; senr. entom., desert locust surv., E.A.H.C., 1949; mem. U.K. del. to inter. locust confs. New Delhi, 1950, Cairo, 1951; author various research pp. on biochem., biolog., and met. subjects.

RAMPHAL, J. I.—b. 1903; ed. Berbice High Sch., B. Guiana; barrister-at-law, Gray's Inn; inspr., labr., 1943; dep. comsnr., labr., 1952; comsnr., 1954; M.L.C.; chmn., sugar industry labr. welf. fund comtee.

RAMSAY, N. J. G.—b. 1916; ed. Merchiston Castle Sch. and Edin. Univ.; nav. serv., 1940-46, lt.; adminr.-gen., N. Rhod., 1947.

RAMSAY, W. H.—b. 1915; ed. Fettes' Coll. and Caius Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1939-42; cadet, Nig., 1938; admin. offr., cl. II, Cyp., 1952; cl. I, 1954; senr. admin. offr., 1954.

RANCE, Maj.-Gen. Sir Hubert, G.C.M.G. (1948), G.B.E. (1946), C.B. (1946), O.B.E. (1940),—b. 1898; ed. Wimbledon Coll. and R.M.C., Sandhurst; mil. serv., 1916-18 and 1939-45, maj.-gen. (desps.); dep. dir., mil. training, W.O.; dir. of tech. training, W.O.; chief of staff to G.O.C.-in-C., Western cmd., Eng., 1943-44; maj.-gen. in charge of civil affrs., Burma, 1945; gov., Burma, 1946; chmn., Br. Caribbean standing closer assoc. comtee.; Br. co-chmn., Caribbean comsn., and chmn., D.W.W.I., 1948; gov. and c.-in-c., Trin., 1950.

RANDALL, J. B.—b. 1913; ed. Bedford Sch., Camb. Univ., and Royal (Dick) Vet. Coll., Edin.; mil. serv., 1940-43, lieut.; vet. offr., Uga., 1939; D.D.V.S., 1949; D.V.S., 1953.

RANFURLY, 6th Earl of, K.C.M.G. (1955).—b. 1913; ed. Eton and Trinity Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1939-45, p.o.w.; A.D.C., gov.-gen., Australia, 1936-39; gov. and c.-in-c., Bah., 1953.

RANGELEY, W. H. J.—b. 1909; ed. Diocesan Coll., Rondebosch, S.A., Plumtree Sch., S. Rhod., Rhodes Univ. Coll., S.A., and Brasenose Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1940-43, capt.; admin. offr., Nyasa, 1934; prov. comsnr., 1952.

RANKINE, Sir John, K.C.M.G. (1954), C.M.G.—b. 1907; ed. Christ's Coll., Christchurch, N.Z., and Exeter Coll., Oxford; cadet, Uga., 1931; asst. dist. comsnr., 1933; asst. sec., E.A.G.C., 1939; asst. col. sec., Fiji, 1942; col. sec., Barb., 1945; ch. sec., Ken., 1947; Br. res., Zanz., 1952; gov., W. reg., Nig., 1954.

RANSLEY, G. J.—b. 1900; ed. Garrison Sch.; postal clk., Malta, 1919; sub. inspr. of police, 1931; inspr., 1939; dir., approved sch., 1941; secon. reg. protection offr., 1942-44; dir., prisons, 1944.

RAPIER, B. A.—b. 1924; ed. Grenada Boys' Secondary Sch., McGill and Edin. Univs.; dist. M.O., Grenada, 1951.

RAWLINGS, G. S.—b. 1904; ed. Rossall, Gonville and Caius Coll., Camb.; cadet, S.S., 1929; mag., etc., Malacca, 1932; 4th mag., S'pore, 1933; ag. res., Labuan, 1933; ag. asst. sec., res., Perak, 1934; cl. IV, A.D.O., K. Kangsar, 1936; 3rd mag., S'pore, 1938; state treas. and pres., town bd., K. Bahru, 1939; cl. III, 1941; Br. min. of infn., N. Delhi, 1942-45; F.E. bureau, F.O., 1946; dep. comsnr., N. Taiping, 1946; cl. II, asst. supt., census, Larut and Metang, 1947; D.O., Larut, 1948; collr., land rev., Malacca, 1948; cl. IB, p.a.s., 1949; dev. offr., R.I.D.A., Selangor, 1951; ag. Br. advr., N. Sembilan, 1953; Kedah, 1954.

REA, J. T.—b. 1907; ed. Royal Sch., Dungannon, Queen's Univ., Belfast, and Camb. Univ.; cadet, F.M.S., attd. Chinese prot., K.L., 1931; cl. V, cl. IV, 1934; cl. III, 1937; cl. II, 1942; dep. comsnr., labr., Kedah, 1946, Penang, 1947; asst. sec., Chinese affrs., 1948; Fed. of Mal., 1949; dep. Mal. estab. offr., cl. IC, 1950; cl. IB, 1951; dep. pres., city coun., S'pore, 1952.
READ, A. T.—b. 1909; ed. Risley Ave., Sec. Sch., London; mil. serv., 1939–45; B.B.C., 1934–37; programme asst., dept. of b'casting, S'pore, 1946; station manager, Penang, 1951; dep. dir., b'casting, S'pore, 1952; Fed. of Mal., 1954.

REDDAWAY, A. F. J.—b. 1916; ed. County Sch., Ilford, and Reading Univ.; admin. offr., Cyp., 1938; cl. I, 1951; senr. admin. offr., 1954; sec., comms. and works, 1955.

REDHEAD, G. A.—b. 1898; ed. Grenada Boys' Sec. Sch.; solr., sup. ct., Wind. and Leeward Is.; dist. offr., N. dist., and mag., Dominica, 1944.

REDHEAD, W. A.—b. 1909; ed. Grenada Boys' Sec. Sch.; clk., agric. dept., Grenada, 1926; treasy. and cust., 1932; clk., cl. I, audit dept., 1945; asst. soc. welf. offr., 1946; i/c publicity servs. during and after loc. disturbances, 1951; supt., prisons, 1952; Barb., 1954.

REDMAN, Lt.-Gen. Sir Harold, K.C.B. (1953), C.B. (1947), C.B.E. (1944).—b. 1899; ed. Farnham and R.M.A., Woolwich; comsmd., R.A., 1917; trans. to K.O.Y.L.I., 1929; brevet maj., 1935; brevet lt.-col., rep. War Cab. sect in France, 1939; battalion cmd., 1940; B.G.S., 8th army, 1941; col., 1942; Br. sec., comb. chiefs of staff (brig.), 1943; maj.-gen., and dep. head, S.H.A.E.F. mission to France, 1944; head of Br. mil. mission to France, 1945; C.G.S. (later C.O.S.), allied land forces, S.E. Asia, 1946; dir., mil. ops., W.O., 1948; prin. S.O. to dep. sup. allied comdr., Europe, 1951; lt.-gen. and V.C.I.G.S., 1952; appdtd. gov. and c.-in-c., Gib., 1955.

REECE, C. W.—b. 1899; ed. Harrison Coll., Codrington Coll., Barb., and Jesus Coll., Oxford; barrister-at-law; police mag., Barb., 1926; registr., 1931; mag., Nig., 1938; cr. coun., 1939; senr., 1946; puisne judge, 1949; H.K., 1952.

REED, C. G.—b. 1906; ed. Christ's Hosp. and King's Coll., Lond.; mil. serv., 1941–46, maj.; divisional educ. offr., Surrey, 1946; dir., educ., Barb., 1947.

REEVE, W. H.—b. 1906; ed. Ossett Gram. Sch. and Univ. of Leeds; mil. serv., 1940–43, capt.; asst. field geol., Tang., 1935; Nyasa., 1943; engrn.-geol., P.W.D., Ken., 1943; senr. engrn.-geol., 1949; ch. geol., dept. labr. and mines, N. Rhod., 1951; dir., geol. surv., 1952; various geol. and scientific publications.

REEVES, B. A.—b. 1914; ed. Tahunau prim. sch. and Nelson Coll., N.Z.; naval serv., 1942–46; surv., Sarawak, 1939; supt., lands and survs., 1947; settlement offr., 1949; surv., N. Borneo, 1950; dep. dir., lands and survs., 1954.

REEVES, E. R.—b. 1909; ed. R.M.C., Duntroon, and Peterhouse, Camb.; cadet, Nig., 1932; p.s. to lieut.-gov., S. Nig., 1934; asst. dist. offr., 1935; asst. dist. comsnr., Pal., 1938; asst. sec. (and clk. to exec. and advsy. couns.), 1943; dep. dist. comsnr., Haifa, 1945; dist. comsnr., Samaria, 1947; secon. C.O., 1949; hd., U.K. claims mission to Israel, 1950; prin. asst. fn. sec., S'pore, 1952.

REGAN, C. L.—b. 1901; ed. Riverview Sch. and Sydney Univ.; dist. mag., Fiji, 1949; senr. mag., 1953.

REID, A. C.—b. 1915; ed. Fettes Coll., Edin., and Camb. Univ.; admin. offr., Fiji, cl. II, 1938; cl. I, 1954; registr., co-op., 1954.

REILLY Sir Bernard, K.C.M.G. (1934), C.I.E. (1926), O.B.E. (1918).—b. 1882; ed. Bedford; joined Indian army, 1902; Indian polit. dept., 1908; held various appts. in India and Aden, officiated as polit. res., Aden, 1925 and 1926; res. and c.-in-c., 1930 and 1931; ch. comsnr., 1932; gov. and c.-in-c., 1937; attd. M.E. dept., C.O., 1940; temp. admin. offr., 1944; accom. Br. del. to refugees conf. at Berm., 1943; mem. of Br. del. to Ethiopia which concluded Anglo-Ethiopian agreement, 1944; hd. of W.O. working party sent to report on ex-Italian cols. in E.A., 1946–47; chmn., Br. Empire soc. for the blind since 1950.

RENISON, Sir Patrick, K.C.M.G. (1955), C.M.G. (1950).—b. 1911; ed. Uppingham Sch. and Corpus Christi Coll., Camb.; C.O., 1932; cadet, Ceylon, 1935; p.s. to gov., 1936; off. asst. to govt. agt., Central Prov., 1938; asst. govt. agt., Kandy, 1939; sec. to fin. sec., and asst. contriv., fin. and supply, 1940; asst. ch. sec., 1940; asst. govt. agt., Trincomalee, 1942; secon. ag. prin., C.O., 1944; visited Italy, Egypt, Iraq, India, Burma, Mal., Ceylon, N. Borneo, Sarawak, Aust., N.Z., and Can., 1944–45; rep. dir. of recruitment (col. serv.) in India, 1945–46; asst. sec., C.O., 1947; col. sec., Trin., 1948; gov. and c.-in-c., B. Hond., 1952.

RENNER, E. A., O.B.E. (1948).—b. 1894; ed. Wesleyan Boys High Sch., S.L., Parkfield Sch., Liverpool, and Edin. Univ.; med. offr., S.L., 1920; pathol., 1934; S.M.O., 1940; asst. D.M.S., 1948; D.D.M.S., 1949; D.M.S., 1952.

RENNIE, A. B.—b. 1896; ed. Wolmer's Sch., J'ca, King's Coll., Lond., and Gray's Inn; barrister-at-law; mil. serv., 1916–19, lieut.; clk. of cts. and ag. res. mag., J'ca, 1929; res. mag., 1933; cr. solr., 1934; puisne judge, 1949; proper offr. of the crown.

RENNIE, Sir Gilbert, G.B.E. (1954), K.C.M.G. (1949), Kt. Bach. (1946), C.M.G. (1941), M.C. (1917).—b. 1895; ed. Stirling High Sch. and Glasgow Univ.; mil. serv., 1915–19, ag. bde.-maj.; cadet, Ceylon, 1920; off. asst. to govt. agt., E. Province, 1921; police mag., 1923; addl. dist. judge, 1925; admin. sec. to D.M.S.S., 1926; contriv., fin. and supply, 1932; sec. to gov., 1934; fin. sec., G.C., 1937; ch. sec., Ken., 1939; ch. sec. and mem. for dev. and recon., 1945; O.A.G., periods, 1942–47; chmn., dev. comte., Ken., 1945; gov. and c.-in-c., N. Rhod., 1948; high comsnr. in U.K. for Fedn. of Rhod. and Nyasa, 1954.
RENNIE, J. S., O.B.E. (1955).—b. 1917; ed. Hillhead High Sch., Glasgow Univ., and Balliol Coll., Oxford; cadet, Tang., 1940; asst. dist. offr., 1942; dist. offr., 1949; dep. col. sec., Maur., 1951.

RENWICK, H. L., O.B.E. (1950).—b. 1898; brill. star of Zanz., IV cl.; Sultan's jib. med.; mil. serv., 1915-19; ch. clk., H.M.T.C., E.A., 1919; asst. immig. and emig. offr., Ken., 1920; asst., H.H. cust., Zanz., 1920; senr. collr., cust., Tang., 1936; asst. compt., 1943; compt., Zanz., 1945; chmn., trade and tour. traff. comtee.

REYNARD, H.—b. 1915; ed. Queen's Park Sch. and Royal Tech. Coll., Glasgow; mil. serv., maj., R.E.; water engr., Leeward Is., 1951.

REYNOLDS, F. G.—b. 1914; ed. King Edward VI Sch. and Univ., Birm.; asst. acctnt., treasy., Nig., 1942; assessment offr., inc. tax dept., G.C., 1945; senr., 1946; asst. comsnr., 1948; dep. comsnr., 1954.

REYNOLDS, J.—b. 1908; ed. Royal Acad. and Queen's Univ., Belfast, Gray's Inn; barrister-at-law, Inns of Court, N.I.; mil. serv., 1941, p.o.w., 1942-45; cr. coun., H.K., 1940; dist. judge, 1953.

RHIND, D., O.B.E.—b. 1899; ed. Aldenham Sch. and Bristol Univ.; mil. serv., 1917-18, mil. admin., Burma, 1946-47, lt.-col.; prof., agric. botany, Rangoon Univ., 1935-42; econ. botanist, Burma, 1943; dir., agric., Ceylon, 1947; sec., agric. and forestry res., W.A.I.T., sec't., 1951; sec., col. agric. res., C.O., 1953; The Grasses of Burma and pp. mainly on rice.

RICE, J. A.—b. 1904; ed. Clongowes and Trinity Coll., Dublin; solr.; asst. comsnr., lands, G.C., 1927; cust. of enemy property, 1939-40; asst. comsnr., lands, Ken., 1947.

RICH, S. M.—b. 1909; ed. Plumtree Sch., S. Rhod.; clk., N. Rhod., 1930; asst. acctnt., 1936; dep. acctn.-gen., Maur., 1943; asst. treas., Cyp., 1948; acctn.-gen., 1948; dir., organization, 1954; sec., orgn. and methods, 1954.

RICHARDS, C. A. L.—b. 1911; ed. Clifton and Brasenose Coll., Oxford; mil. serv., 1939-41, maj.; spec. duties, 1941-46; cadet, Uga., 1934; dist. offr., 1946; comsnr. for soc. dev., Tang., 1950; community dev., Uga., 1953; res., Buganda, 1954.

RICHARDS, C. G.—b. 1908; ed. privately; C.M.S., 1935; advr. on lit. for Africans to E.A. govts., 1947; dir., E.A. lit. bureau, E.A.H.C., 1948; reader in Swahili to inter. languages comtee., 1945-49; author of papers on book distributing in E.A. in Towards a Literate Africa.

RICHARDS, H. G., O.B.E. (1948).—b. 1904; ed. St. John's Sch., Leatherhead, Imp. Coll. of Sci. and New Coll., Oxford; cadet, Tang., 1929; asst. sec., E.A.G.C., 1937 (attd. E.A. Force H.Q., Sept.-Nov., 1939); 1st asst. sec., Cyp., 1941; ch. asst. sec., 1942; und. sec., 1948; cadet, cl. I., H.K., dep. col. sec., 1951; chmn., urb. coun., 1954.

RICHARDS, N. F.—b. 1914; ed. Barry Gram. Sch., and Bristol Univ.; mil. serv., 1940-44; P.W.D., Swaz., 1944; div. engmt., P.W.D., N. Rhod., 1947; D.P.W., Som., 1948; Nyasa., 1951.

RICHARDSON, E. R.—b. 1912; ed. Calabar High Sch., Kingston, J'ca; asst., constab., dept., J'ca, 1933; senr. clk., 1943; prin., 1944; asst. treas., 1944; asst. sec., 1947; prin. asst. sec., 1950; perm. sec., 1953; secon., C.O., 1953; under sec. (finance), J'ca, 1955; sec., land policy co-ord. and hurricane insurance comtees.

RICHARDSON, M. W.—b. 1911; mil. serv., 1942-45; Br. P.O., 1928; asst. surv., 1936; asst. prin., 1939; p.s. to asst. P.M.G., 1941; dir. of traffic, Ken., Uga. and Tang., posts and tels. dept., 1946; asst. P.M.G. (services), E.A.P. & T., 1952.

RICHARDSON, P. A.—b. 1918; ed. Rugby and Cambridge Univ.; barrister-at-law; mil. serv., 1940-43; admin. cadet, Nig., 1940; admin. offr., cl. IV, 1943; secon., Tonga, sec. to govt., 1947; sec. to govt., B.S.I.P., 1951; secon. C.O., 1953.

RIDEHALGH, A., Q.C.—b. 1907; ed. Sedbergh Sch. and Wadham Coll., Oxford; barrister-at-law; cr. atty. and mag., St. Kitts, 1935; cr. coun., G.C., 1939; solr.-gen., Nig., 1946; atty.-gen., H.K., 1952.

RIDLEY, N. C. A.—b. 1909; ed. Charterhouse and Trinity Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law, Inner Temple; mil. serv., 1940-47, lt.-col.; cadet, N. Rhod., 1932; dist. offr., 1934; asst. sec., 1950; chmn. of comtees., 1951.

RIGBY, I. C. C.—b. 1911; ed. Magdalen Coll. RINGWOOD, E., O.B.E. (1952).—b. 1891; entd. C.A. offr., 1910; mil. serv., 1916-19; asst. hd., pay dept., 1927; dep. hd., engnr. contracts, 1938; hd., pay dept., 1945.

ROBERTS, A. W.—b. 1904; ed. Stettler High Sch., Alberta, Queens Univ., and Univ. of Toronto; mil. serv., 1940-46, sqdn. ldr.; asst. dir. educ., Barb., 1946; prin., Erdiston teacher train. coll., 1947; author of reports of select comtees. on govt. indust. schs. and voc. and tech. educ.

ROBERTS, L. A.—b. 1910; ed. Dominica Gram. Sch.; asst. clk., P.W.D., Dominica, 1933; clk. various depts., 1933-45; social welf. offr., 1945.

ROBERTS-WRAY, Sir Kenneth, K.C.M.G. (1949), C.M.G. (1946).—b. 1899; ed. R.M.A., Woolwich, 1917-18; comsmd., Jan., 1918; mil. serv., 1918-20; Merton Coll., Oxford; called to bar, 1924; S.E. circuit; professional legal clk., min. of health, 1926; ag. asst. ch. clk., 1929; 2nd asst. legal advr., D.O. and C.O., 1931; asst. legal advr., 1943; legal advr., 1945;
chmn., law offrs. conf., Trin., 1944, and jud. advrs.; conf., Uga., 1953; part author of The Law of Collisions on Land.

ROBERTSHAW, F. D.—b. 1914; ed. Auckland Gram. Sch. and Univ. Coll.; barrister and solr., sup. ct., N.Z.; mil. serv., 1939-44; res. mag., Som., 1951.

ROBERTSON, A. R. W., C.M.G. (1953), C.B.E. (1948), E.D.—b. 1902; cd. Dean Close Sch., Cheltenham; mil. serv., 1943, capt.; asst. treas., G.C., 1929; dep. treas., B. Guiana, 1936; acctnt.-gen., etc., Fiji, 1939; fin. sec., 1940; gov's dep. on various occasions since 1943; fin. sec., Trin., 1948; ag. gov., 1952; fin. sec., Nig., 1953; Fed. of Nig., 1954; chmn., Fiji and W. Pac. H.C., supply and prodn. bd., 1943-46; spec. duty in connexion with defence and supply to N.Z., Aus., Washington and London, 1941-44; deleg., civ. aviation confce., Wellington, 1946; advr., S. Pac. reg. confce., Canberra, 1947; chmn., Trin. and Tob. election comsn., 1949-50; chmn., B. Carib. currency comsn., 1951-52.

Rhod., 1930; examr. of acct., 1936; acctnt., 1940; senr. acctnt., Nig., 1946; prin., 1949; treas., E. regn., 1951; acctnt.-gen., 1954; contrlr., acctns. and stats. and of imports, N. Rhod., 1943-45.

ROBERTSON, P. A. P.—b. 1913; ed. King's Coll. Choir Sch., Sedbergh Sch. and King's Coll., Camb.; cadet, Tang., 1936; asst. dist. offr., 1938; clk. of ex. and leg. couns., 1945-46; dist. offr., 1948; prin. asst. sec., 1949; fin. sec., Aden, 1951.

ROBERTSON, S. D. M.—b. 1912; ed. Roslin Publ. Sch., Dalkeith High Sch., and Heriot-Watt Tech. Coll.; war serv., civ. garrison engrn., 1939-43; asst. engrn., G.C. rwy., 1943; ch. engrn., S.L., 1950.

ROBINS, G. H., M.B.E.—b. 1909; ed. Bromsgrove Sch.; asst. supt., police, Cey., 1930; supt., 1943; senr., Tang., 1949; asst. comsnr., 1951; dep. comsnr., 1953; comsnr., police, Cyp., 1954.

ROBINSON, G. G.—b. 1894; ed. Repton and Trinity Coll., Oxford; barrister-at-law, Inner Temple; mil. serv., 1914-19, capt. and adjt.; res. mag., Ken., 1930; puisne judge, N. Rhod., 1938; Nig., 1947; ch. just., Zanz., 1952; mem., Rhod. ct. of appeal; chmn., comsns. of pub. enq., conciliator, indust. disputes, N. Rhod.

ROBINSON, H. K.—b. 1906; ed. St. Bees Sch., Cumberland, and Peterhouse, Camb.; cadet, Nig., 1929; secon. to Cams. plantations, 1940-41; secon. to labour dept., 1943-46; admin. offr., cl. II, 1949; cl. I, 1952.

ROBINSON, P. B.—b. 1901; ed. Wesley Coll., Dublin and Dublin Univ.; med. offr., N. Rhod., 1926; S.M.O., 1939; D.D.M.S., Uga., 1945; D.M.S., N. Rhod., 1947; mem., comn. on African hous. in urb. areas, N. Rhod., 1944.

ROBISON, L. McD., C.B.E. (1942)—b. 1886; ed. Grace Ramsden's Sch. and Manchester Univ.; mil. serv., 1918; lect., govt. training coll., Colombo, 1909; vice-prin., 1912; inspr., schs., 1915; asst. dir., educ., 1924; dep. dir., 1929; sec. to min. of educ., 1932; dir., educ., 1936; asst. educ. advr., C.O., 1945.

ROBSON, I. G.—b. 1899; mil. serv., 1917-19; entd. C.A. off., 1920; hr. exec. offr., pay dept., 1943; senr. exec. offr., shipping dept., 1948; stores dept., 1951; dep. hd. pay dept., 1952.

ROBSON, M.—b. 1908; ed. Geo. Heriot's and Edin. and Oxford Univs.; mil. serv., 1941-45, capt.; asst. conserv., forests, Nig., 1930; senr. asst., 1945; conserv., 1949; ch. conserv., 1953.

RODDAN, G. McM.—b. 1906; ed. Dumfries Acad., Glasgow and Oxford Univs., and I.C.T.A.; agric. offr., S.L., 1930; asst. agric. advr., C.O., 1943; dep., 1947; dep. dir., agric., Ken., 1948; dir., Nyasa., 1950; Ken., 1951.

RODGERS, C. G. H.—b. 1916; ed. County Sch., Whitby, Queen's Coll., Camb.; mil. serv., 1940-46, flt.-lt.; cadet engnr., K.U.R. & H., 1937; asst. engnr., 1940; dist. engnr., E.A.R. & H., 1950; senr., 1952; planning engnr., 1954.

RODWAY, R.—b. 1908; ed. King Edward VI Sch., Retford, and King's Coll., Camb.; asst. audr., S.L., 1931; Nig., 1934; B. Guiana, 1936; Trin., 1942; senr. asst. audr., Ken., 1945; dep. audr. (later dep. dir., aud.), 1946; prin. audr., N. Borneo, 1951.

ROE, F. W.—b. 1913; ed. Gram. Sch., Hitchin, and Reading Univ.; war serv., 1941-42, R.A.F. spec.-pilot, 1945-46, lt.-col.; geol., Mal., 1937; Uga., 1943; Mal., 1947; dir., geol. surv., Br. Borneo terrs., 1949.

ROE, H. S.—b. 1901; ed. Wellington Coll., Berks. and King's Coll., Lond.; asst. engnr., P.W.D., Tang., 1927; div. engnr., gr. II, 1942; div. engnr., gr. I, 1944; D.D.P.W., Fiji, 1947; Sarawak, 1952.

ROSS, C.—b. 1910; ed. Antigua Gram. ROSS, P. S.—b. 1918; ed. City of London Sch. and Merton Coll., Oxford; secon. from H.M. Treas. as fin. sec., B. Hond., 1952.

ROTH, G. K., O.B.E. (1954).—b. 1903; ed. Heath Gram. ROWE, E. G.—b. 1904; ed. Chatham Hse. Sch., Ramsgate, and St. Edmund Hall, Oxford; cadet, Tang., 1928; asst. dist. offr., 1930; dist. offr., 1940; dep. prov. comsnr., 1947; prov. comsnr., 1948; senr., 1952; author of The Breeding Biology of "Aquila Verreauxii" Lesson (Ibis), July and Oct., 1947.

RUDD, G. B. W.—b. 1908; ed. St. Columba's Coll. and Trinity Coll., Dublin; barrister-at-law, King's Inn, Dublin; res. mag., Ken., 1936; ch. just., Aden (secon.), 1944; judge of the sup. ct., Aden, 1945; puisne judge, Ken., 1951; author of Kenya Law Reports, 1936-42, and Eastern Africa Court of Appeal Law Reports, 1936-42.

RULF, G. H., O.B.E. (1944).—b. 1903; ed. Hastings Gram. Sch. and Lond. Sch. of Econ.; mil. serv., 1940-46, colonel; cont. offr., econ. cont. bd., Tang., 1948; prov. prod. offr., 1949; asst. dir., grain storage, 1950; dir., 1951.

RUTLEDGE, G. A. L.—b. 1913; mil. serv., 1940-42; Tang., 1935-49; acctnt., Basuto., 1949; treas., Bech. Prot., 1950.

SCOTT, K. B., M.B.E. (Mil.)—b. 1917; ed. Merchant Taylors' Sch.; mil. serv., 1939-46, lieut.-comdr.(S), R.N.V.R. (desps.); cadet, Nig., 1946; admin. offr., cl. IV., 1949; cl. III., 1951; govt. sec., St. H., 1954.

WIGGINS, W. D. C.—b. 1905; ed. Dragon Sch., Oxford, King's Sch., Canterbury, and Univ. Coll., Lond.; mil. serv., 1939-46, lt.-col.; survey dept., Nig., 1928; asst. dir., col. (geodetic & topog.), surv., 1946; dep. dir., 1948.

WILKINSON, H. D. D.—b. 1909; ed. Leeds Gram. Sch., Woodhouse Grove Sch., Paris and Camb., Univs.; asst.

WILSON, D. B., O.B.E. (1953).—b. 1901; ed. Monkton Combe and Camb. and Birm. Univs.; mil. serv., 1939-46, lt.-col.; med. offr., Tang., 1929; malariologist, 1946; E.A.H.C., 1949.

WILSON, Sir Mark, Kt. Bach. (1950).—b. 1896; ed.
