ind., Mal., 1954; H.K. rep. on F.E. econ. and supplies comtee., C.O., 1945; H.K. del., S.E. Asia reg. conf. on stats., 1948; comsnr. of regisn., 1949; H.K. rep. conf. of col. govt. statisticians, Lond., 1950.

WRAY, M. O., O.B.E. (1954).—b. 1912; ed. St. George's Sch., Harpenden and Wadham Coll., Oxford; admin. offr., Uga., 1935; admin. sec., Zanz., 1949; asst. ch. sec. to the high comsnr. for Basuto., Bech. Prot. and Swaz., 1952; admin. sec., 1953.

WREFORD, C. K.—b. 1906; ed. Cranleigh and Clare Coll., Camb.; barrister-at-law, Middle Temple; cadet, Hyg., 1928; admin. offr., cl. III., 1931; cl. II., 1946; cl. I., 1949; staff gr., 1953.

WRIGHT, J., C.B.E. (1954).—b. 1904; ed. Dunfermline High Sch., St. Andrews and Edin. Univs., East of Scotland Coll. of Agric. and I.C.T.A.; mil. serv., 1939-42; capt.; mycologist, G.C., 1929; inspr., plants and produce, 1931; asst. supt., agric., 1933; supt., 1936; senr. agric. offr., 1945; J'ca., 1946; dep. dir., agric. (research), 1949; dir., agric., 1951; served on war emerg. comtees., Ashanti, 1942-45; author of papers on botany, plant path., entom., and gen. agric. in sci. journals.

WRIGHT, R. A.—b. 1912; ed. Roborough, Eastbourne and R.Vet. Coll., Lond.; mil. serv., 1944-46; vet. offr., S.S., 1937; attd. c'wealth health dept., Canberra, 1942; vet. offr., Ken. (secon.), 1943; dep. food contrlr., S'pore, 1945; state vet. offr., Johore, 1946; ch. vet. offr., S'pore, 1950.

WYLIE, A. D. B.—b. 1909; ed. Leith Acad.; mil. serv., 1939-45; press supt., Tang., 1949; asst. govt. printer, 1952; govt. printer, 1953.

WYLIE, C., Q.C., E.D.—b. 1905; ed. Auckland Gram. Sch. and N.Z. Univ.; barrister, Inner Temple; mil. serv., 1941-46; maj. (desps.); cr. coun., Mal., 1946; senr. fedl. coun., 1950; atty.-gen., Barb., 1951.

WYN-HARRIS, Sir Percy, K.C.M.G. (1950), M.B.E.—b. 1903; ed. Gresham's Sch., Holt, and Caius Coll., Camb.; cadet, Ken., 1926; settlement offr. (Kikuyu land claims), 1939-40; labr. liaison offr., 1944; prov. comsnr., 1945; ch. native comsnr., 1947; gov. and c-in-c., Gam., 1949.

WYN-PUGH, L.—b. 1910; ed. Gram. Sch., Dolgelly; mil. serv., 1939-46; local govt., U.K., 1931-39; exec. engnr., gr. II., Tang., 1946; gr. I., 1947; asst. D.P.W., 1950; D.P.W., G.C., 1952.

YAACOB BIN ABDUL LATIF—b. 1918; ed. Victoria Instn., Kuala Lumpur; M.C.S., 1937; secon. state P.R.O., N. Sembilan; senr. infn. offr., 1948; asst. dir., infn. servs., Fed. of Mal., 1952; dep. dir.-gen., 1953; dir., 1954.

YARNOLD, D. H.—b. 1908; ed. Cheltenham Gram. Sch.; mil. serv., 1939-46; capt.; engng. appts., Wolverhampton and Sheffield Corps., 1930-37; exec. engnr., P.W.D., Nig., 1937; D.D.P.W., Gam., 1951; D.P.W., 1953.

YEO, Kok Cheang.—b. 1903; ed. H.K. and Camb. Univs.; asst. M.O.H., H.K., 1928; lectr. and examr. in public health, H.K. Univ., 1936-37; Chinese health offr., senr. gr., 1939; dep. dir., health serv., 1947; dep. dir., med. and health serv., 1950; dir., 1952.

YORK, A. D.—b. 1907; ed. The King's Hospital and Trinity Coll., Dublin; interned 1942-45; cadet, M.C.S., 1929; cl. V., 1932; res., Labuan, 1934; cl. IV., 1936; D.O., 1938; cl. III., 1941; cl. II., 1946; res. comsnr., cl. IB., 1948; p.a.s., comsnr.-gen., S.E. Asia, 1950; ag. pres., munic. coun., K. Lumpur, 1952.

YOUENS, P. W.—b. 1916; ed. King Edward VII Sch. and Wadham Coll., Oxford; naval serv., 1939-40, sub-lt.; cadet, S.L., 1939; asst. dist. comsnr., 1942; dist. comsnr., 1948; asst. sec., Nyasa., 1951; dep. ch. sec., 1953; com. H.Q. judicial and Free-town police dists., 1950; M.L.C., 1950 and 1954.

YOUNG, A. H., C.B.E.—b. 1899; ed. George Watson's Coll., Edin., and Edin. Univ.; mil. serv., 1917-19, 2nd lieut.; inspr., produce, Nig., 1926; dep. suptg. inspr., 1927; suptg. inspr., 1928; dep. dir. of commerce and industry, 1945; dir., marketing and exports dept., 1948; chmn., Cameroons Devpt. Corp., 1952, of fed. mktg. bds. in addn., 1954; General History of Produce Inspection in Nigeria, 1938.

YOUNG, G. L., O.B.E. (1950).—b. 1903; ed. Sandys Gram. Sch. and Lyceum Sch., Berm.; clk., Berm., 1917; asst. col. treas., 1930; audr., 1952.

YOUNG, R. M.—b. 1900; ed. Mountjoy Sch. and Dublin Univ.; p.o.w., 1942-45; European mstr., S.S., 1924; headmstr., 1929; supvr. private schs., S'pore, 1938; prin., Anderson Sch., Ipoh, 1946; dep. dir., educ., S'pore, 1946; dir., 1952.

YOUNG, W. M.—b. 1917; ed. Wellington Coll. and Camb. Univ.; interned, 1942-45; cadet, M.C.S., 1939; cl. V., 1942 (retros.); cl. IV., 1946; asst. comsnr., labr., Mal., 1948; cl. III., 1950; ag. estab. offr., S'pore, 1953.

ZAMMIT, J.—b. 1898; ed. St. Aloysius Coll. and Royal Univ. of Malta; sub. clk., 1919; civ. serv. (admin.), Malta, 1922; dir., printing office, 1946.

ZANNETIDES, C.—b. 1898; Licencié en Droit, Academie d'Aix, Univ. d'Aix, Marseilles; barrister-at-law, Gray's Inn; off. receiver in bankruptcy, 1940; dist. judge, Cyp., 1941; pres., dist. ct., 1949.

ZEKIA, M.—b. 1903; ed. Univ. of Istanbul; barrister-at-law, Middle Temple; dist. judge, Cyp., 1940; pres., dist. ct., 1947; puisne judge, 1952; comsnr. of enq. for Lefkonico incidents, 1945; chmn., comtee. on Turkish affrs., 1948.

ZENON, L. E.—b. 1903; Licencié en Droit, Univ. of Paris; barrister-at-law, Middle Temple; dist. judge, Cyp., 1942; pres., dist. ct., 1952.
PART IV
PARLIAMENTARY AND NON-PARLIAMENTARY PAPERS OF COLONIAL INTEREST PUBLISHED DURING 1954

Arranged in three parts: (a) periodicals, (b) by subject, (c) by territories.

(For earlier papers see The Colonial Office List 1951 to 1954).

Parliamentary Papers:

Cmd. Command Paper
H.C. House of Commons
T.S. Treaty Series

Non-Parliamentary Papers:

Col. Colonial numbered series
N.P.P. Un-numbered H.M.S.O. publications
S.I. Statutory Instruments

(a) PERIODICALS

Colonial Geology and Mineral Resources; the quarterly bulletin of the Colonial Geological Surveys. Vol. 1, No. 1, 1950-. Annual subscription £1 11s.

Colonial Plant and Animal Products; the quarterly journal of the Colonial Products Laboratory Vol. 1, No. 1, 1950-. Annual subscription £1 1s.

Corona; the journal of Her Majesty's Oversea Service. Monthly. Vol 1, No. 1, February 1949-. Annual subscription £1.

Digest of Colonial Statistics. Bi-monthly. No. 1, March-April, 1952-. Annual subscription £1 11s. 6d.

Empire Survey Review. Quarterly. Vol. 1, No. 1, 1931-. Published by the Crown Agents for Oversea Governments and Administrations.

Journal of African Administration. Quarterly. Vol. 1, No. 1, 1949-. Annual subscription 10s. 6d.

Oversea Education; a journal of educational experiment and research in tropical and sub-tropical areas. Quarterly. Vol. 1, No. 1, 1929. Annual subscription 8s.

Today; a pictorial magazine relating to colonial life and development. Published every six weeks for the Colonial Office by the Central Office of Information. 7s. for 12 issues.
